, but the factor loading matrices and factor series require further identification due to the multiplicative form. Without loss of generality, write

\widetilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}=\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{M}_{r}+\bm{\Gamma}_{r},\;\;\;\text{where }\bm{\Gamma}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{r}=\mathbf{0},

with
\mathbf{M}_{r}\in\mathbbm{R}^{k_{r}\times k_{r}}
and
\bm{\Gamma}_{r}\in\mathbbm{R}^{p\times k_{r}}
, but can have zero columns. Then we have

\mathbf{0}=\bm{\Gamma}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{Q}_{c}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}=\bm{\Gamma}_{r}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{Z,t}\widetilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}=\bm{\Gamma}_{r}^{\prime}\bm{\Gamma}_{r}\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{Z,t}\widetilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{c},

which can only be true in general if
\bm{\Gamma}_{r}=\mathbf{0}
since
\widetilde{\mathbf{F}}_{t}
is random and
\widetilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}^{\prime}\widetilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}\to\bm{\Sigma}_{A,c}
due to (L1). Using (L1),
\mathbf{M}_{r}
is of full rank and hence
\widetilde{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}
and
\mathbf{Q}_{r}
share the same column space. Similarly, the factor loading space of
\mathbf{Q}_{c}
is identified, and
\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}
is hence identified once
\mathbf{Q}_{r}
and
\mathbf{Q}_{c}
are given correspondingly.

\square

Proof of Theorem
2
.
By Assumption (IC1), we have
\mu_{t}=\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}(\mathbf{Y}_{t}-\mathbf{E}_{t})\mathbf{1}_{q}/pq
and hence

(\widehat{\mu}_{t}-\mu_{t})^{2}=\frac{1}{p^{2}q^{2}}\Big(\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\Big)^{2}=\frac{1}{p^{2}q^{2}}\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{q}E_{t,ij}\Big)^{2}.

(6.1)

Assumption (E1) implies each entry of
\mathbf{E}_{t}
has zero mean and bounded fourth moment, and we have

\mathbbm{E}\bigg[\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{q}E_{t,ij}\Big)^{2}\bigg]=\text{Var}\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{q}E_{t,ij}\Big)=\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\sum_{h=1}^{q}\text{cov}(E_{t,ij},E_{t,lh})=O(pq),

(6.2)

where we used Lemma
1
in the last equality. Thus with (
6.1
),
(\widehat{\mu}_{t}-\mu_{t})^{2}=O_{P}(p^{-1}q^{-1})
.

Similar to the rate for
\widehat{\mu}_{t}
, by again (IC1) we have
\bm{\alpha}_{t}=q^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}-\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}-q^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}
and
\bm{\beta}_{t}=p^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}-\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}-p^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p}\cdot\|\widehat{\bm{\alpha}}_{t}-\bm{\alpha}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p}\cdot\Big\|(\mu_{t}-\widehat{\mu}_{t})\mathbf{1}_{p}+q^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\Big\|^{2},

(6.3)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{q}\cdot\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{t}-\bm{\beta}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{q}\cdot\Big\|(\mu_{t}-\widehat{\mu}_{t})\mathbf{1}_{q}+p^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\Big\|^{2}.

(6.4)

From (
6.1
) we have
\|(\mu_{t}-\widehat{\mu}_{t})\mathbf{1}_{p}\|^{2}=O_{P}(q^{-1})
and
\|(\mu_{t}-\widehat{\mu}_{t})\mathbf{1}_{q}\|^{2}=O_{P}(p^{-1})
. Furthermore, by Lemma
1
,

\mathbbm{E}\Big[\|q^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\|^{2}\Big]=q^{-2}\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{p}\text{Var}\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{q}E_{t,ij}\Big)=O(pq^{-1}).

Similarly,
\|p^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\|^{2}=O_{P}(qp^{-1})
. Then we have (
6.3
) and (
6.4
) as

\frac{1}{p}\cdot\|\widehat{\bm{\alpha}}_{t}-\bm{\alpha}_{t}\|^{2}=O_{P}(q^{-1}),\;\;\;\frac{1}{q}\cdot\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{t}-\bm{\beta}_{t}\|^{2}=O_{P}(p^{-1}).

In the rest of the proof, we show consistency of the factor loading estimators. From (
3.4
), we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{Y}_{t}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}-q^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t},

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}-q^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime},

and hence the following decomposition

\begin{split}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}&=\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}-q^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\\
&+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}+(pq)^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\\
&-q^{-1}(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}-p^{-1}(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\\
&-q^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}-q^{-1}(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}+q^{-2}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}+q^{-1}p^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\\
&-p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}-(pq)^{-1}p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}+q^{-1}p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}+p^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\\
&=\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-q^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&-(pq)^{-1}p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}+p^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\\
&=\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathcal{Q}_{1}-\mathcal{Q}_{2}-\mathcal{Q}_{3}-\mathcal{Q}_{4}-\mathcal{Q}_{5}+\mathcal{Q}_{6}+\mathcal{Q}_{7},\;\;\;\text{where}\\
\mathcal{Q}_{1}&:=(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime},\;\;\;\mathcal{Q}_{2}:=p^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime},\;\;\;\mathcal{Q}_{3}:=q^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime},\;\;\;\mathcal{Q}_{4}:=p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime},\\
\mathcal{Q}_{5}&:=(pq)^{-1}p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime},\;\;\;\mathcal{Q}_{6}:=(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime},\;\;\;\mathcal{Q}_{7}:=p^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.5)

From (
3.1
), we have
\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}=pq\mu_{t}+\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}
and
\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}=q\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+q\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}
. Thus,

\begin{split}\mathcal{Q}_{1}&=(pq)^{-1}(pq^{2}\mu_{t}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+pq^{2}\mu_{t}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+pq\mu_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+q\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+q\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime})\\
&=q\mu_{t}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+q\mu_{t}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mu_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.6)

Similarly, we have
\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}=(\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}+\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{C}_{t}+\mathbf{E}_{t})(\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{q}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})
. Further with Assumption (IC1),

\begin{split}\mathcal{Q}_{2}&=p^{-1}(\mu_{t}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&+\mu_{t}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&+\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&+\mu_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{C}_{t}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&+\mu_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\\
&=q\mu_{t}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+q\mu_{t}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\\
&+\mathbf{C}_{t}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mu_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.7)

Since
\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}=q\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}+q\bm{\alpha}_{t}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}
, we have

\begin{split}\mathcal{Q}_{3}&=q^{-1}(q\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}+q\bm{\alpha}_{t}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q})(q\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+q\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})\\
&=q\mu_{t}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+q\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+q\mu_{t}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+q\bm{\alpha}_{t}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+\mu_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+q^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.8)

Similar to (
6.7
), we have

\begin{split}\mathcal{Q}_{4}&=q\mu_{t}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+q\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\\
&+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&+\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\\
&+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&+p^{-1}\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&=q\mu_{t}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+q\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&+p^{-1}\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime},\end{split}

(6.9)

where the last equality used Assumption (IC1). For
\mathcal{Q}_{5}
and
\mathcal{Q}_{6}
, we have

\begin{split}\mathcal{Q}_{5}&=(pq)^{-1}p^{-1}(pq\mu_{t}+\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q})(pq\mu_{t}+\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p})\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\\
&=q\mu_{t}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+(pq)^{-1}p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime},\end{split}

(6.10)

and

\begin{split}\mathcal{Q}_{6}&=(\mu_{t}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p})(q\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+q\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})\\
&=q\mu_{t}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+q\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.11)

Lastly for
\mathcal{Q}_{7}
, we have similar to (
6.7
) that

\begin{split}\mathcal{Q}_{7}&=p^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}(\mu_{t}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&+\mu_{t}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&+\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&+\mu_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{C}_{t}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&+\mu_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\\
&=p^{-2}(pq\mu_{t}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+pq\mu_{t}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+p\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+p\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+p\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&+\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\\
&=q\mu_{t}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\\
&+p^{-1}\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.12)

With (
6.6
), (
6.7
), (
6.8
), (
6.9
), (
6.10
), (
6.11
), and (
6.12
), we have

\begin{split}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}&=\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{Y}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathcal{Q}_{1}-\mathcal{Q}_{2}-\mathcal{Q}_{3}-\mathcal{Q}_{4}-\mathcal{Q}_{5}+\mathcal{Q}_{6}+\mathcal{Q}_{7}\\
&=q\mu_{t}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+q\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+q\mu_{t}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+q\bm{\alpha}_{t}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{C}_{t}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+\mu_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&+q\mu_{t}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+q\mu_{t}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\mu_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\\
&-q\mu_{t}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-q\mu_{t}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\\
&-\mathbf{C}_{t}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-\mu_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-\mathbf{E}_{t}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-q\mu_{t}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-q\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&-\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-q\mu_{t}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-q\bm{\alpha}_{t}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-\mu_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}-q^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&-q\mu_{t}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-q\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}-\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}-\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\\
&-p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-q\mu_{t}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\\
&-p^{-1}\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-(pq)^{-1}p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+q\mu_{t}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\\
&+q\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\alpha}_{t}^{\prime}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&+q\mu_{t}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-1}\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\\
&+p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\bm{\beta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\\
&=\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&-p^{-1}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\\
&-(pq)^{-1}p^{-1}(\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p})^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.13)

Swapping the roles of row and column factor loadings, we can arrive at similarly

\begin{split}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}&=\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}+\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}+\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}+\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\\
&-q^{-1}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}-q^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}-q^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}-q^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}-q^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\\
&-(pq)^{-1}q^{-1}(\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q})^{2}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}+q^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.14)

For ease of notation, we define

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{r,t}

\displaystyle:=\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}

\displaystyle-p^{-1}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}

\displaystyle-(pq)^{-1}p^{-1}(\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p})^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}+p^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime},

(6.15)

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{c,t}

\displaystyle:=\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}+\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}+\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}

\displaystyle-q^{-1}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}-q^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}-q^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}-q^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}-q^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}

\displaystyle-(pq)^{-1}q^{-1}(\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q})^{2}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}+q^{-2}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime},

(6.16)

so that from (
6.13
) and (
6.14
), we can write

\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}=\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{R}_{r,t},\;\;\;\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}=\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{C}_{t}+\mathbf{R}_{c,t}.

Recall that we denote by
\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}
the
k_{r}\times k_{r}
diagonal matrix with the first largest
k_{r}
eigenvalues of
T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}
on the main diagonal, and since
\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}
consists of the corresponding eigenvectors, we have

\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}=T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}.

(6.17)

With (
6.13
) and
\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}=\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{Q}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}
, we can write the
j
-th row of estimated row factor loading as

\begin{split}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,j\cdot}&=T^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime})_{ij}\\
&=T^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,i\cdot}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{Q}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}^{\prime})\mathbf{Q}_{r,j\cdot}+T^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{R}_{r,t})_{ij}.\end{split}

Thus with the definition
\mathbf{H}_{r}=T^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{r}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{Q}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}^{\prime})
, we have

\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,j\cdot}=T^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{R}_{r,t})_{ij},

and hence we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}-\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\prime}\|_{F}^{2}=\sum_{j=1}^{p}\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,j\cdot}\|^{2}=\sum_{j=1}^{p}\Big\|T^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{r,t}\Big)_{\cdot j}\Big\|^{2}\\
&\leq T^{-2}\cdot\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{r,t}\Big\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}\Big(T^{-1}p^{2(1-\delta_{r,k_{r}})}q^{1-2\delta_{c,1}}+p^{1-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{2(1-\delta_{c,1})}\Big),\end{split}

where the last equality used Lemma
2
and Lemma
3
. The consistency of
\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}
can be similarly shown (omitted here). This completes the proof of Theorem
2
.
\square

Proof of Theorem
3
.

From (
3.4
), we can first write

\begin{split}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}&=\mathbf{Y}_{t}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}-q^{-1}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\\
&=\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}+\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{Q}_{c}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}\\
&+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}(\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}+\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{Q}_{c}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t})\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\\
&-q^{-1}(\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}+\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{Q}_{c}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t})\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\\
&-p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}(\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}+\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{Q}_{c}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t})\\
&=\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}+\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}+\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{Q}_{c}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\\
&+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{Q}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\\
&-q^{-1}\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}-q^{-1}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}-q^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}-q^{-1}\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{Q}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}-q^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\\
&-p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mu_{t}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\bm{\alpha}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\bm{\beta}_{t}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{Q}_{c}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\\
&=\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{Q}_{c}^{\prime}+\mathbf{E}_{t}+(pq)^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}-q^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}-p^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t},\end{split}

where the last equality used Assumption (IC1). Thus, we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{Z,t}-(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{-1}=\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}-(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{-1}\\
&=\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}(\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\prime})(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{-1}(\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{\prime})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}-(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{-1}+\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}\\
&+(pq)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}-q^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}-p^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}\\
&=\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}(\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\prime}-\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r})(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{-1}(\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{\prime}-\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}+\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}(\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\prime}-\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r})(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{-1}\\
&+(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{-1}(\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{\prime}-\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}+(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}-\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{H}_{r})^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}-\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{\prime})\\
&+(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}-\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{H}_{r})^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{\prime}+\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}-\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{\prime})+\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{\prime}\\
&+(pq)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}-q^{-1}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}-\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\prime})^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}-q^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}\\
&-p^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}-\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{\prime})-p^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{\prime}\\
&=:\mathcal{I}_{F,1}+\mathcal{I}_{F,2}+\mathcal{I}_{F,3}+\mathcal{I}_{F,4}+\mathcal{I}_{F,5}+\mathcal{I}_{F,6}+\mathcal{I}_{F,7}+\mathcal{I}_{F,8}\\
&-\mathcal{I}_{F,9}-\mathcal{I}_{F,10}-\mathcal{I}_{F,11}-\mathcal{I}_{F,12},\;\;\;\text{where}\end{split}

(6.18)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{F,1}:=\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}(\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\prime}-\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r})(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{-1}(\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{\prime}-\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c},

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{F,2}:=\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}(\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\prime}-\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r})(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{-1},\;\;\;\mathcal{I}_{F,3}:=(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{-1}(\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{\prime}-\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c},

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{F,4}:=(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}-\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{H}_{r})^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}-\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{\prime}),\;\;\;\mathcal{I}_{F,5}:=(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}-\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{H}_{r})^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{\prime},

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{F,6}:=\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}-\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{\prime}),\;\;\;\mathcal{I}_{F,7}:=\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{\prime},\;\;\;\mathcal{I}_{F,8}:=(pq)^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c},

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{F,9}:=q^{-1}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}-\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\prime})^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c},\;\;\;\mathcal{I}_{F,10}:=q^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c},

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{F,11}:=p^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}-\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{\prime}),\;\;\;\mathcal{I}_{F,12}:=p^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{\prime}.

First consider
\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}
, by its definition and Assumption (L1) we have

\|\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\|_{F}^{2}\leq\|\mathbf{F}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{Z}_{c}^{1/2}\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}(p^{\delta_{r,1}}q^{\delta_{c,1}}).

Then for
\mathcal{I}_{F,1}
, we have

\begin{split}\|\mathcal{I}_{F,1}\|_{F}^{2}&=O_{P}(p^{\delta_{r,1}}q^{\delta_{c,1}})\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\prime}-\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{\prime}-\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}\|_{F}^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\big(T^{-2}p^{2-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{2-2\delta_{c,k_{c}}}+T^{-1}p^{3-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{2-\delta_{c,1}-3\delta_{c,k_{c}}}\\
&\hskip 12.0pt+T^{-1}q^{3-2\delta_{c,k_{c}}}p^{2-\delta_{r,1}-3\delta_{r,k_{r}}}+p^{3-\delta_{r,1}-3\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{3-\delta_{c,1}-3\delta_{c,k_{c}}}\big),\end{split}

where we used Lemma
6
in the last equality. Similarly, we can obtain the following for
\mathcal{I}_{F,2}
and
\mathcal{I}_{F,3}
,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{I}_{F,2}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\big(T^{-1}p^{1+2\delta_{r,1}-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{1-\delta_{c,1}}+p^{1+\delta_{r,1}-3\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{2-\delta_{c,1}}\big),

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{I}_{F,3}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\big(T^{-1}q^{1+2\delta_{c,1}-2\delta_{c,k_{c}}}p^{1-\delta_{r,1}}+q^{1+\delta_{c,1}-3\delta_{c,k_{c}}}p^{2-\delta_{r,1}}\big).

For
\mathcal{I}_{F,4}
, from Assumptions (E1) and (E2) we easily have
\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}=O(pq)
, so we have

\begin{split}\|\mathcal{I}_{F,4}\|_{F}^{2}&=O_{P}(pq)\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\prime}-\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{\prime}-\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c}\|_{F}^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\big(T^{-2}p^{3-\delta_{r,1}-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{3-\delta_{c,1}-2\delta_{c,k_{c}}}+T^{-1}p^{4-\delta_{r,1}-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{3-2\delta_{c,1}-3\delta_{c,k_{c}}}\\
&\hskip 12.0pt+T^{-1}q^{4-\delta_{c,1}-2\delta_{c,k_{c}}}p^{3-2\delta_{r,1}-3\delta_{r,k_{r}}}+p^{4-2\delta_{r,1}-3\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{4-2\delta_{c,1}-3\delta_{c,k_{c}}}\big),\end{split}

For
\mathcal{I}_{F,5}
, consider first

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\mathbbm{E}\Big\{\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{A}_{c}\|_{F}^{2}\Big\}\leq pk_{c}\max_{i\in[p],j\in[k_{c}]}\mathbbm{E}\Big\{(\mathbf{E}_{t,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c,\cdot j})^{2}\Big\}\\
&=pk_{c}\max_{i\in[p],j\in[k_{c}]}\sum_{n=1}^{q}\sum_{l=1}^{q}\text{cov}(E_{t,in},E_{t,il})\cdot A_{c,nj}A_{c,lj}=O(pq),\end{split}

(6.19)

where the last equality used Assumptions (E1) and (E2). Thus,

\begin{split}\|\mathcal{I}_{F,5}\|_{F}^{2}&=O_{P}(pq)\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\prime}-\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{Z}_{c}^{-1/2}\|_{F}^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\big(T^{-1}p^{2+\delta_{r,1}-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{2-2\delta_{c,1}-\delta_{c,k_{c}}}+p^{2-3\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{3-2\delta_{c,1}-\delta_{c,k_{c}}}\big),\end{split}

where we used Lemma
6
and Assumption (L1). Similarly for
\mathcal{I}_{F,6}
,

\|\mathcal{I}_{F,6}\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}\big(T^{-1}q^{2+\delta_{c,1}-2\delta_{c,k_{c}}}p^{2-2\delta_{r,1}-\delta_{r,k_{r}}}+q^{2-3\delta_{c,k_{c}}}p^{3-2\delta_{r,1}-\delta_{r,k_{r}}}\big).

By Assumptions (E1) and (E2) again, we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\mathbbm{E}\Big\{\|\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{A}_{c}\|_{F}^{2}\Big\}\leq k_{r}k_{c}\max_{i\in[k_{r}],j\in[k_{c}]}\mathbbm{E}\Big\{(\mathbf{A}_{r,\cdot i}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{A}_{c,\cdot j})^{2}\Big\}\\
&=k_{r}k_{c}\max_{i\in[k_{r}],j\in[k_{c}]}\sum_{m=1}^{p}\sum_{n=1}^{q}\sum_{h=1}^{p}\sum_{l=1}^{q}\text{cov}(E_{t,mn},E_{t,hl})\cdot A_{r,mi}A_{c,nj}A_{r,hi}A_{c,lj}=O(pq),\end{split}

hence for
\mathcal{I}_{F,7}
, it holds that

\|\mathcal{I}_{F,7}\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}(pq)\cdot\|\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{-1/2}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{Z}_{c}^{-1/2}\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}\big(p^{1-\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{1-\delta_{c,k_{c}}}\big).

Consider
\mathcal{I}_{F,8}
, recall that
(\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q})^{2}=O_{P}(pq)
from (
6.2
) and hence

\|\mathcal{I}_{F,8}\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}(p^{-2}q^{-2})\cdot(\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q})^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}(1).

For
\mathcal{I}_{F,9}
, note that
\mathbbm{E}[\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\|_{F}^{2}]
has the same rate as (
6.19
) since
k_{c}
is fixed, and hence

\begin{split}\|\mathcal{I}_{F,9}\|_{F}^{2}&=O_{P}(q^{-2})\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\prime}-\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{1}_{q}\|^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\big(T^{-1}p^{2+\delta_{r,1}-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{1-2\delta_{c,1}}+p^{2-3\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{2-2\delta_{c,1}}\big).\end{split}

From (
6.2
), we also have
\mathbbm{E}[\|\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\|_{F}^{2}]=O(pq)
, so for
\mathcal{I}_{F,10}
we have

\|\mathcal{I}_{F,10}\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}(q^{-2})\cdot\|\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{-1/2}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{1}_{q}\|^{2}=O_{P}\big(p^{1-\delta_{r,k_{r}}}\big).

Lastly, the rates for
\mathcal{I}_{F,11}
and
\mathcal{I}_{F,12}
can be obtained similarly as
\mathcal{I}_{F,9}
and
\mathcal{I}_{F,10}
,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{I}_{F,11}\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}\big(T^{-1}q^{2+\delta_{c,1}-2\delta_{c,k_{c}}}p^{1-2\delta_{r,1}}+q^{2-3\delta_{c,k_{c}}}p^{2-2\delta_{r,1}}\big),

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{I}_{F,12}\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}\big(q^{1-\delta_{c,k_{c}}}\big).

Therefore, with all the rates from
\mathcal{I}_{F,1}
to
\mathcal{I}_{F,12}
in (
6.18
), by using Assumption (R1) we have

\begin{split}\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{Z,t}-(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}&=O_{P}\big(p^{1-\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{1-\delta_{c,k_{c}}}+T^{-1}p^{1+2\delta_{r,1}-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{1-\delta_{c,1}}+p^{1+\delta_{r,1}-3\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{2-\delta_{c,1}}\\
&\hskip 36.0pt+T^{-1}q^{1+2\delta_{c,1}-2\delta_{c,k_{c}}}p^{1-\delta_{r,1}}+q^{1+\delta_{c,1}-3\delta_{c,k_{c}}}p^{2-\delta_{r,1}}\big).\end{split}

This shows the first statement of Theorem
3
.

For the remaining proof, consider
\widehat{C}_{t,ij}-C_{t,ij}
for any
t\in[T],i\in[p],j\in[q]
. First, we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\widehat{C}_{t,ij}-C_{t,ij}=\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{Z,t}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c,j\cdot}-\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{Q}_{c,j\cdot}\\
&=(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,i\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot})^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{Z,t}-(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{-1})(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{c}\mathbf{Q}_{c,j\cdot})\\
&+(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,i\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot})^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{Z,t}-(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{-1})\mathbf{H}_{c}\mathbf{Q}_{c,j\cdot}+(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,i\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot})^{\prime}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{-1}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{c}\mathbf{Q}_{c,j\cdot})\\
&+(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,i\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot})^{\prime}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{Q}_{c,j\cdot}+\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{Z,t}-(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{-1})(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{c}\mathbf{Q}_{c,j\cdot})\\
&+\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{Z,t}-(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{-1})^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{-1})\mathbf{H}_{c}\mathbf{Q}_{c,j\cdot}+\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{-1}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{c}\mathbf{Q}_{c,j\cdot}).\end{split}

(6.20)

Notice that from Assumption (L1),

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\|^{2}=O(\|\mathbf{A}_{r,i\cdot}\|^{2})\cdot\|\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{-1/2}\|_{F}^{2}=O(p^{-\delta_{r,k_{r}}}),

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{Q}_{c,j\cdot}\|^{2}=O(\|\mathbf{A}_{c,j\cdot}\|^{2})\cdot\|\mathbf{Z}_{c}^{-1/2}\|_{F}^{2}=O(q^{-\delta_{c,k_{c}}}).

Together with Lemma
6
, the first statement of Theorem
3
and Assumption (R1), we have

\begin{split}(\widehat{C}_{t,ij}-C_{t,ij})^{2}&=O_{P}\big(p^{1-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{1-2\delta_{c,k_{c}}}+T^{-1}p^{1+2\delta_{r,1}-3\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{1-\delta_{c,1}-\delta_{c,k_{c}}}+p^{1+\delta_{r,1}-4\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{2-\delta_{c,1}-\delta_{c,k_{c}}}\\
&\hskip 36.0pt+T^{-1}q^{1+2\delta_{c,1}-3\delta_{c,k_{c}}}p^{1-\delta_{r,1}-\delta_{r,k_{r}}}+q^{1+\delta_{c,1}-4\delta_{c,k_{c}}}p^{2-\delta_{r,1}-\delta_{r,k_{r}}}\big).\end{split}

This completes the proof of Theorem
3
.
\square

Proof of Theorem
4
.

We first consider
\widehat{\mu}_{t}
, which is given by

\displaystyle\widehat{\mu}_{t}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}/(pq)=\mu_{t}+\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}/(pq)=\mu_{t}+\frac{1}{pq}\sum_{i,j}E_{t,ij},\text{ so that }

\displaystyle\widehat{\mu}_{t}-\mu_{t}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{pq}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,r}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}\mathbf{A}_{e,c}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}+\frac{1}{pq}\sum_{i,j}\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}\epsilon_{t,ij}=:I_{\mu,1}+I_{\mu,2}.

By Assumption (E1), since
\|\mathbf{A}_{e,r}\|_{1},\|\mathbf{A}_{e,c}\|_{1}=O(1)
, we have
I_{\mu,1}=O_{P}(1/(pq))
. Also,
I_{\mu,2}=O((pq)^{-1/2})
since
\mathbbm{E}(I_{\mu,2})=0
and
\textnormal{Var}(I_{\mu,2})=(pq)^{-2}\sum_{i,j}\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}^{2}=O(1/(pq))
. Hence
I_{\mu,1}
is dominated by
I_{\mu,2}
, and

\displaystyle\sqrt{pq}(\widehat{\mu}_{t}-\mu_{t})

\displaystyle=\sqrt{pq}I_{\mu,2}(1+o_{P}(1))=\frac{1}{\sqrt{pq}}\sum_{i,j}\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}\epsilon_{t,ij}(1+o_{P}(1))\xrightarrow{\mathcal{D}}\mathcal{N}(0,\gamma_{\mu}^{2}),

where we use Theorem 1 in
Ayvazyan and Ulyanov, (2023)
for the convergence in distribution.

For
\widehat{\bm{\alpha}}_{t}
, consider the decomposition

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\alpha}}_{t}-\bm{\alpha}_{t}

\displaystyle=(\mu_{t}-\widehat{\mu}_{t})\mathbf{1}_{p}+q^{-1}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}

\displaystyle=(\mu_{t}-\widehat{\mu}_{t})\mathbf{1}_{p}+q^{-1}\mathbf{A}_{e,r}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}\mathbf{A}_{e,c}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}+q^{-1}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}\circ\bm{\epsilon}_{t})\mathbf{1}_{q},\text{ so that }

\displaystyle\widehat{\alpha}_{t,i}-\alpha_{t,i}

\displaystyle=(\mu_{t}-\widehat{\mu}_{t})+q^{-1}(\mathbf{A}_{e,r})_{i\cdot}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}\mathbf{A}_{e,c}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}+q^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}\epsilon_{t,ij}.

From what we have proved above we have
|\widehat{\mu}_{t}-\mu_{t}|=O_{P}(1/\sqrt{pq})
. Also,
q^{-1}(\mathbf{A}_{e,r})_{i\cdot}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}\mathbf{A}_{e,c}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{q}=O_{P}(1/q)
since
\|\mathbf{A}_{e,r}\|_{1},\|\mathbf{A}_{e,c}\|_{1}=O(1)
. Finally,
\mathbbm{E}(q^{-1}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}\circ\bm{\epsilon}_{t})\mathbf{1}_{q})=\mathbf{0}
and
\textnormal{Var}(q^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}\epsilon_{t,ij})=q^{-2}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}^{2}=O(q^{-1})
, implying that
q^{-1}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}\circ\bm{\epsilon}_{t})\mathbf{1}_{q}=O_{P}(q^{-1/2})
elementwise, thus dominating other terms. Hence for
i\in[p]
, using Theorem 1 in
Ayvazyan and Ulyanov, (2023)
,

\displaystyle\sqrt{q}(\widehat{\alpha}_{t,i}-\alpha_{t,i})

\displaystyle=q^{-1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}\epsilon_{t,ij}(1+o_{P}(1))\xrightarrow{\mathcal{D}}\mathcal{N}(0,\gamma_{\alpha,i}^{2}).

By Assumption (E1), each element of
q^{-1}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}\circ\bm{\epsilon}_{t})\mathbf{1}_{q}
is independent of each other. Hence with integers
i_{1}<\cdots<i_{m}
,
m
being finite and
\bm{\theta}_{\alpha,t}:=(\alpha_{t,i_{1}},\ldots,\alpha_{t,i_{m}})^{\prime}
, by Theorem 1 in
Ayvazyan and Ulyanov, (2023)
,

\displaystyle\sqrt{q}(\widehat{\bm{\theta}}_{\alpha,t}-\bm{\theta}_{\alpha,t})\xrightarrow{\mathcal{D}}\mathcal{N}(\mathbf{0},\text{diag}(\gamma_{\alpha,i_{1}}^{2},\ldots,\gamma_{\alpha,i_{m}}^{2})).

We omit the proof of asymptotic normality for
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{t}
since the arguments used are in parallel to those used for
\widehat{\bm{\alpha}}_{t}
, using the independence of the columns in
\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}\circ\bm{\epsilon}_{t}
by Assumption (E1).

The rest of the proof is done if we can prove that
\widehat{\gamma}_{\alpha,i}
,
\widehat{\gamma}_{\beta,j}
and
\widehat{\gamma}_{\mu}
are consistent estimators for
\gamma_{\alpha,i}
,
\gamma_{\beta,j}
and
\gamma_{\mu}
respectively. From (
3.6
), since we assume
\widehat{C}_{t,ij}-C_{t,ij}=o_{P}(1)
from Theorem
3
, then elementwise we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{t}=(\mu_{t}-\widehat{\mu}_{t})\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}+(\bm{\alpha}_{t}-\widehat{\bm{\alpha}}_{t})\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{p}(\bm{\beta}_{t}-\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{t})^{\prime}+(\mathbf{C}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{t})+\mathbf{E}_{t}=\mathbf{E}_{t}(1+o_{P}(1)).

Hence we have

\displaystyle q^{-1}(\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{t}^{\prime})_{ii}

\displaystyle=\{q^{-1}(\mathbf{A}_{e,r})_{i\cdot}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}\mathbf{A}_{e,c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}^{\prime}(\mathbf{A}_{e,r})_{i\cdot}^{\prime}+q^{-1}(\mathbf{A}_{e,r})_{i\cdot}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}\mathbf{A}_{e,c}^{\prime}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}\circ\bm{\epsilon}_{t})_{i\cdot}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\quad+q^{-1}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}\circ\bm{\epsilon}_{t})_{i\cdot}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}\circ\bm{\epsilon}_{t})_{i\cdot}^{\prime}\}(1+o_{P}(1))

\displaystyle=O_{P}(q^{-1})+O_{P}(q^{-1})+\frac{1}{q}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}^{2}(\epsilon_{t,ij}^{2}-1)(1+o_{P}(1))+\frac{1}{q}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}^{2}

\displaystyle\xrightarrow{\mathcal{P}}\gamma_{\alpha,i}^{2},

where we used the Markov inequality to arrive at
q^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}^{2}(\epsilon_{t,ij}^{2}-1)=O_{P}(q^{-1/2})
, knowing that each
\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}
is bounded away from infinity by Assumption (E1). A parallel argument (omitted) can show that
\widehat{\gamma}_{\beta,j}^{2}
is consistent for
\gamma_{\beta,j}^{2}
. Finally,

\displaystyle\widehat{\gamma}_{\mu}^{2}=p^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}q^{-1}(\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{t}^{\prime})_{ii}\xrightarrow{\mathcal{P}}p^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\gamma_{\alpha,i}^{2}=\gamma_{\mu}^{2}.

This completes the proof of the theorem.
\square

Proof of Theorem
5
.

We construct the asymptotic normality for rows of our factor loading estimators. We only prove the result for the row loading estimator, and the proof for the column loading estimator would be similar (and hence omitted). For any
j\in[p]
, consider the following decomposition

\begin{split}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,j\cdot}&=T^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{R}_{r,t})_{ij}\\
&=T^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,i\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot})\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{R}_{r,t})_{ij}+T^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{R}_{r,t})_{ij}\\
&=\mathcal{I}_{1}+\mathcal{I}_{2}+\mathcal{I}_{3}+\mathcal{I}_{4}+\mathcal{I}_{5}-\mathcal{I}_{6}-\mathcal{I}_{7}-\mathcal{I}_{8}-\mathcal{I}_{9}-\mathcal{I}_{10}-\mathcal{I}_{11}+\mathcal{I}_{12}+\mathcal{I}_{13},\;\;\;\text{where}\end{split}

(6.21)

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{1}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{T}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})_{ij},\;\;\;\mathcal{I}_{2}:=\frac{1}{T}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime})_{ij},

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{3}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{T}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})_{ij},\;\;\;\mathcal{I}_{4}:=\frac{1}{Tpq}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime})_{ij},

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{5}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{Tpq}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})_{ij},\;\;\;\mathcal{I}_{6}:=\frac{1}{Tp}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime})_{ij},

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{7}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{Tp}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime})_{ij},\;\;\;\mathcal{I}_{8}:=\frac{1}{Tp}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime})_{ij},

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{9}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{Tp}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})_{ij},\;\;\;\mathcal{I}_{10}:=\frac{1}{Tp}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})_{ij},

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{11}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{Tp^{2}q}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}((\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p})^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime})_{ij},\;\;\;\mathcal{I}_{12}:=\frac{1}{Tp^{2}}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime})_{ij},

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{13}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{T}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,i\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot})\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{R}_{r,t})_{ij}.

We shall show that
\mathcal{I}_{1}
is the leading term among the decomposition in (
6.21
). To obtain the rate for
\mathcal{I}_{2}
, from Assumptions (E1) and (E2) we have for any
i\in[p],h\in[q]
,

E_{t,ih}=\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot}+(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon})_{ih}\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,w}(\mathbf{X}_{\epsilon,t-w})_{ih}.

Consider first
\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{q}(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{A}_{e,k,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,h\cdot})\mathbf{A}_{c,h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r,j\cdot}
. By Assumptions (F1), (E1) and (E2), we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\mathbbm{E}\Big\{\Big(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{q}(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{A}_{e,k,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,h\cdot})\mathbf{A}_{c,h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r,j\cdot}\Big)^{2}\Big\}\\
&=\text{cov}\Big(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{q}\mathbf{A}_{c,h\cdot}^{\prime}(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{f,w}\mathbf{X}_{f,t-w}^{\prime})\mathbf{A}_{r,j\cdot}(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{A}_{e,k,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,h\cdot}),\\
&\hskip 36.0pt\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{q}\mathbf{A}_{c,h\cdot}^{\prime}(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{f,w}\mathbf{X}_{f,t-w}^{\prime})\mathbf{A}_{r,j\cdot}(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{A}_{e,k,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,h\cdot})\Big)\\
&=\sum_{h=1}^{q}\sum_{l=1}^{q}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{f,w}^{2}a_{e,w}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{r,j\cdot}\|^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{c,h\cdot}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{c,l\cdot}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{e,c,l\cdot}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}\|^{2}\\
&=O(T)\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{r,j\cdot}\|^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}\|^{2}.\end{split}

(6.22)

Consider also
\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{h=1}^{q}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}((\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon})_{ih}\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,w}(\mathbf{X}_{\epsilon,t-w})_{ih})\mathbf{A}_{c,h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r,j\cdot}
. Similarly, by Assumptions (E1), (E2) and (F1), we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\mathbbm{E}\Big\{\Big\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{h=1}^{q}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}((\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon})_{ih}\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,w}(\mathbf{X}_{\epsilon,t-w})_{ih})\mathbf{A}_{c,h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r,j\cdot}\Big\|^{2}\Big\}\\
&=\text{cov}\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{h=1}^{q}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}((\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon})_{ih}\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,w}(\mathbf{X}_{\epsilon,t-w})_{ih})\mathbf{A}_{c,h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r,j\cdot},\\
&\hskip 36.0pt\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{h=1}^{q}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}((\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon})_{ih}\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,w}(\mathbf{X}_{\epsilon,t-w})_{ih})\mathbf{A}_{c,h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r,j\cdot}\Big)\\
&=\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{h=1}^{q}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{f,w}^{2}a_{\epsilon,w}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{r,j\cdot}\|^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{c,h\cdot}\|^{2}\cdot(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon})_{ih}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\|^{2}\\
&=O(T)\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{r,j\cdot}\|^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{c}\|^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{r}\|^{2}.\end{split}

(6.23)

Hence using Lemma
3
, it holds that

\begin{split}\|\mathcal{I}_{2}\|^{2}&\leq\frac{1}{T^{2}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\Big\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{q}E_{t,ih}\mathbf{A}_{c,h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r,j\cdot}\Big\|^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\Big(\frac{1}{T^{2}}p^{-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{-2\delta_{c,1}}\Big)\Big\{\Big\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{h=1}^{q}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}((\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon})_{ih}\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,w}(\mathbf{X}_{\epsilon,t-w})_{ih})\mathbf{A}_{c,h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{j\cdot}\Big\|^{2}\\
&+\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\|\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\|^{2}\Big)\sum_{i=1}^{p}\Big(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{q}(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{A}_{e,k,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,\text{-}k,h\cdot})\mathbf{A}_{c,h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r,j\cdot}\Big)^{2}\Big\}\\
&=O_{P}\Big(\frac{1}{T^{2}}p^{-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{-2\delta_{c,1}}\cdot Tq^{\delta_{c,1}}\Big)=O_{P}\big(T^{-1}p^{-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{-\delta_{c,1}}\big),\end{split}

where we used Assumption (L1), (
6.22
) and (
6.23
) in the last equality.

For
\mathcal{I}_{3}
, first notice from the noise structure in Assumptions (E1) and (E2), we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\text{Var}\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{h=1}^{q}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}\Big)\\
&=O(1)\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{u=1}^{p}\sum_{h=1}^{q}\sum_{l=1}^{q}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=1}^{k_{e,r}}\sum_{m=1}^{k_{e,c}}\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}^{4}A_{e,r,in}A_{e,r,un}A_{e,r,jn}^{2}A_{e,c,hm}^{2}A_{e,c,lm}^{2}\\
&\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{r,u\cdot}\|\cdot\text{Var}((\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w})_{nm}^{2})\\
&+O(1)\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{h=1}^{q}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,w}^{4}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon})_{ih}^{2}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon})_{jh}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\|^{2}\cdot\text{Var}((\mathbf{X}_{\epsilon,t-w})_{ih}(\mathbf{X}_{\epsilon,t-w})_{jh})\\
&=O(T+Tq)=O(Tq).\end{split}

Moreover, it holds that

\mathbbm{E}\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\Big|\sum_{h=1}^{q}\sum_{t=1}^{T}E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}\Big|\Big)=\sum_{i=1}^{p}\Big|\sum_{h=1}^{q}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\big(\|\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot}\|^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{e,r,j\cdot}\|+(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon})_{ih}\mathbbm{1}_{\{i=j\}}\big)\Big|=O(Tq),

and with
\max_{i}\|\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\|^{2}\leq\|\mathbf{A}_{r,j\cdot}\|^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{-1/2}\|^{2}=O_{P}\big(p^{-\delta_{r,k_{r}}}\big)
, we thus have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\|\mathcal{I}_{3}\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{T^{2}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\Big\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{q}E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}\Big\|^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\Big(T^{-2}p^{-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{-2\delta_{c,1}}(Tq+T^{2}q^{2}p^{-\delta_{r,k_{r}}})\Big)=O_{P}\big(T^{-1}p^{-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{1-2\delta_{c,1}}+p^{-3\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{2-2\delta_{c,1}}\big).\end{split}

(6.24)

Consider now
\mathcal{I}_{4}
and
\mathcal{I}_{5}
. From the proof of (
6.37
) in Lemma
2
,

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\|\mathcal{I}_{4}\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{2}q^{2}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\Big\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime})_{ij}\Big\|^{2}\\
&\leq\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{2}q^{2}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\|\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\|^{2}\Big)\sum_{i=1}^{p}\Big(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\Big)_{ij}^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\big(T^{-2}p^{-2-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{-2-2\delta_{c,1}}\big)\cdot\Big\|\Big(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\Big)_{\cdot j}\Big\|^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\big(T^{-2}p^{-2-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{-2-2\delta_{c,1}}\big)\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{p}\mathbbm{E}\Bigg\{\Bigg[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{u=1}^{q}E_{t,iu}\Big)\sum_{h=1}^{q}E_{t,kh}\Bigg]^{2}\Bigg\}\\
&=O_{P}\Big(T^{-2}p^{-2-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{-2-2\delta_{c,1}}(Tp^{2}q^{2}+T^{2}pq^{2})\Big)\\
&=O_{P}\big(T^{-1}p^{-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{-2\delta_{c,1}}+p^{-1-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{-2\delta_{c,1}}\big),\end{split}

where we used Lemma
3
in the third line and (
6.50
) in the second last equality. Similarly, we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\|\mathcal{I}_{5}\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{2}q^{2}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\Big\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})_{ij}\Big\|^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\big(T^{-2}p^{-2-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{-2-2\delta_{c,1}}\big)\cdot\Big\|\Big(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\Big)_{j\cdot}\Big\|^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\big(T^{-2}p^{-2-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{-2-2\delta_{c,1}}\big)\cdot p\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Bigg\{\Bigg[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{u=1}^{q}E_{t,iu}\Big)\sum_{h=1}^{q}E_{t,jh}\Bigg]^{2}\Bigg\}\\
&=O_{P}\Big(T^{-2}p^{-2-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{-2-2\delta_{c,1}}(Tp^{2}q^{2}+T^{2}pq^{2})\Big)\\
&=O_{P}\big(T^{-1}p^{-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{-2\delta_{c,1}}+p^{-1-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{-2\delta_{c,1}}\big),\end{split}

where we used again Lemma
3
in the third line and (
6.50
) in the second last equality.

Consider now
\mathcal{I}_{6}
, note first from Assumptions (E1) and (E2),

E_{t,hu}=\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,u\cdot}+(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon})_{hu}\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,w}(\mathbf{X}_{\epsilon,t-w})_{hu}.

Thus, we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\text{cov}(E_{t,hu},E_{s,vl})=\mathbbm{E}[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,u\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,l\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{e,s}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,v\cdot}]+\mathbbm{1}_{\{h=v\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{u=l\}}\cdot(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon})_{hu}^{2}\cdot\mathbbm{E}[\bm{\epsilon}_{t,\cdot u}^{\prime}\bm{\epsilon}_{s,\cdot l}]\\
&=\mathbf{A}_{e,c,l\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,u\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,v\cdot}\cdot\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}a_{e,w+|t-s|}+\mathbbm{1}_{\{h=v\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{u=l\}}\cdot(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon})_{hu}^{2}\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,w}a_{\epsilon,w+|t-s|}.\end{split}

Hence if we fix
t\in[T],h\in[p],u\in[q]
, then for any deterministic vectors
\mathbf{n}\in\mathbbm{R}^{k_{r}}
and
\mathbf{g},\mathbf{j}\in\mathbbm{R}^{k_{c}}
, we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{v=1}^{p}\sum_{l=1}^{q}\mathbbm{E}\Big[E_{t,hu}\mathbf{n}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{g}\cdot E_{s,vl}\mathbf{j}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{n}\Big]=\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{v=1}^{p}\sum_{l=1}^{q}\text{cov}(E_{t,hu},E_{s,vl})\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbf{n}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{g}\mathbf{j}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{n}\Big]\\
&=\sum_{v=1}^{p}\sum_{l=1}^{q}\Big[O(\mathbf{A}_{e,c,l\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,u\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,v\cdot})\cdot\sum_{w\geq 0}\sum_{m\geq 0}\sum_{s=1}^{T}a_{e,w}a_{e,w+|t-s|}a_{f,m}a_{f,m+|t-s|}\\
&+O(\mathbbm{1}_{\{h=v\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{u=l\}}\cdot(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon})_{hu}^{2})\cdot\sum_{w\geq 0}\sum_{m\geq 0}\sum_{s=1}^{T}a_{\epsilon,w}a_{\epsilon,w+|t-s|}a_{f,m}a_{f,m+|t-s|}\Big]\\
&=\sum_{v=1}^{p}\sum_{l=1}^{q}O(\mathbf{A}_{e,c,l\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,u\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,v\cdot}+\mathbbm{1}_{\{h=v\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{u=l\}}\cdot(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon})_{hu}^{2})=O(1),\end{split}

where for the second last equality, we argue for the first term in the second last line only, as the second term could be shown similarly:

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\sum_{w\geq 0}\sum_{m\geq 0}\sum_{s=1}^{T}a_{e,w}a_{e,w+|t-s|}a_{f,m}a_{f,m+|t-s|}=\sum_{w\geq 0}\sum_{m\geq 0}a_{e,w}a_{f,m}\sum_{s=1}^{T}a_{e,w+|t-s|}a_{f,m+|t-s|}\\
&\leq\sum_{w\geq 0}\sum_{m\geq 0}|a_{e,w}|\cdot|a_{f,m}|\cdot\Big(\sum_{s=1}^{T}a_{e,w+|t-s|}^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\Big(\sum_{s=1}^{T}a_{f,m+|t-s|}^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\leq\sum_{w\geq 0}\sum_{m\geq 0}|a_{e,w}|\cdot|a_{f,m}|\leq c^{2},\end{split}

where the constant
c
is from Assumptions (F1) and (E2). Finally,

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\mathbbm{E}\Big\{\Big(\sum_{h=1}^{p}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})_{ih}\Big)^{2}\Big\}=\mathbbm{E}\Big\{\Big(\sum_{u=1}^{q}\sum_{h=1}^{p}\sum_{t=1}^{T}E_{t,hu}\mathbf{A}_{c,u\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r,i\cdot}\Big)^{2}\Big\}\\
&=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{p}\sum_{u=1}^{q}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{v=1}^{p}\sum_{l=1}^{q}\mathbbm{E}\Big[E_{t,hu}\mathbf{A}_{r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{A}_{c,u\cdot}\cdot E_{s,vl}\mathbf{A}_{c,l\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{s}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r,i\cdot}\Big]=O(Tpq).\end{split}

(6.25)

Thus, we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\|\mathcal{I}_{6}\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{2}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\Big\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime})_{ij}\Big\|^{2}\\
&=\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{2}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\Big\|\sum_{h=1}^{p}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})_{ih}(\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime})_{hj}\Big\|^{2}\\
&\leq\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{2}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{p}\Big(\sum_{h=1}^{p}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})_{ih}\Big)^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\big(T^{-2}p^{-2-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{-2\delta_{c,1}}\cdot Tp^{2}q\big)=O_{P}\big(T^{-1}p^{-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{1-2\delta_{c,1}}\big),\end{split}

where we used Cauchy-Schwarz inequality in the third line, both Lemma
3
and (
6.25
) in the second last equality. Similarly, we have the following for
\mathcal{I}_{7}
,

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\|\mathcal{I}_{7}\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{2}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\Big\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime})_{ij}\Big\|^{2}\\
&=\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{2}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\Big\|\sum_{h=1}^{p}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime})_{ih}(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime})_{hj}\Big\|^{2}\\
&\leq\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{2}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot p\Big(\sum_{h=1}^{p}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})_{jh}\Big)^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\big(T^{-2}p^{-2-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{-2\delta_{c,1}}\cdot Tp^{2}q\big)=O_{P}\big(T^{-1}p^{-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{1-2\delta_{c,1}}\big).\end{split}

For
\mathcal{I}_{8}
and
\mathcal{I}_{9}
, their rates can be shown to be the same as that for
\mathcal{I}_{3}
by the following,

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\|\mathcal{I}_{8}\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{2}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\Big\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime})_{ij}\Big\|^{2}\\
&=\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{2}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\Big\|\sum_{h=1}^{p}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{l=1}^{q}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}E_{t,il}E_{t,hl}\Big\|^{2}\\
&\leq\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{2}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot p\sum_{h=1}^{p}\Big\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{l=1}^{q}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}E_{t,il}E_{t,hl}\Big\|^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\big(T^{-2}p^{-2-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{-2\delta_{c,1}}\cdot p^{2}(Tq+T^{2}q^{2}p^{-\delta_{r,k_{r}}})\big)=O_{P}\big(T^{-1}p^{-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{1-2\delta_{c,1}}+p^{-3\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{2-2\delta_{c,1}}\big),\end{split}

where we used Lemma
3
and (
6.24
) in the second last equality. The proof for
\|\mathcal{I}_{9}\|^{2}
is similar to the above by using the proof of (
6.24
) previously and omitted here.

For
\mathcal{I}_{10}
, first observe from the proof of (
6.50
), we also have for any
j\in[p]
,

\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbbm{E}\Big\{\Big(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{l=1}^{q}E_{t,il}\sum_{h=1}^{q}E_{t,jh}\Big)^{2}\Big\}=O\big(Tpq^{2}+T^{2}q^{2}\big),

then together with Lemma
3
, it holds that

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\|\mathcal{I}_{10}\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{2}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\Big\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})_{ij}\Big\|^{2}\\
&=\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{2}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\Big\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{l=1}^{q}E_{t,il}\sum_{h=1}^{q}E_{t,jh}\Big\|^{2}\\
&\leq\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{2}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{p}\Big(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{l=1}^{q}E_{t,il}\sum_{h=1}^{q}E_{t,jh}\Big)^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\big(T^{-2}p^{-2-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{-2\delta_{c,1}}\cdot(Tpq^{2}+T^{2}q^{2})\big)=O_{P}\big(T^{-1}p^{-1-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{2-2\delta_{c,1}}+p^{-2-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{2-2\delta_{c,1}}\big).\end{split}

Consider now
\mathcal{I}_{11}
, we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\|\mathcal{I}_{11}\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{4}q^{2}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\Big\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p})^{2}\Big\|^{2}\\
&\leq\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{4}q^{2}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot p\Big[\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p})^{2}\Big]^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\big(T^{-2}p^{-4-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{-2-2\delta_{c,1}}\cdot T^{2}p^{3}q^{2}\big)=O_{P}\big(p^{-1-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{-2\delta_{c,1}}\big),\end{split}

where we used Lemma
3
and the rate from (
6.2
) in the second last equality.

For
\mathcal{I}_{12}
, we have

\begin{split}\|\mathcal{I}_{12}\|^{2}&\leq\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{4}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\Big\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\Big\|^{2}\\
&\leq\frac{1}{T^{2}p^{4}}\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{r}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot p\Big(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\Big)^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\big(T^{-2}p^{-4-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{-2\delta_{c,1}}\cdot T^{2}p^{3}q^{2}\big)=O_{P}\big(p^{-1-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{2-2\delta_{c,1}}\big),\end{split}

where the last line used the following result which can be shown similar to (
6.43
),

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\mathbbm{E}\Big\{\Big(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\Big)^{2}\Big\}=\mathbbm{E}\Big\{\Big(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{q}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}\Big)^{2}\Big\}\\
&=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{q}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{l=1}^{q}\sum_{m=1}^{p}\sum_{n=1}^{p}\text{cov}(E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh},E_{s,ml}E_{s,nl})+\Big(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{h=1}^{q}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{p}\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh}]\Big)^{2}\\
