&=O\big(Tp^{2}q^{2}+T^{2}p^{2}q^{2}\big)=O\big(T^{2}p^{2}q^{2}\big).\end{split}

Lastly,
\|\mathcal{I}_{13}\|^{2}
is dominated by the terms from
\mathcal{I}_{1}
to
\mathcal{I}_{12}
using Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and Theorem
2
. We require the term
\mathcal{I}_{1}
to be truly dominating by using Assumption (AD1) and we equivalently compare the rates without the term
\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}
. Notice the rates for
\|\mathcal{I}_{2}\|^{2}
,
\|\mathcal{I}_{4}\|^{2}
,
\|\mathcal{I}_{5}\|^{2}
,
\|\mathcal{I}_{6}\|^{2}
,
\|\mathcal{I}_{7}\|^{2}
,
\|\mathcal{I}_{10}\|^{2}
,
\|\mathcal{I}_{11}\|^{2}
and
\|\mathcal{I}_{12}\|^{2}
are bounded above by the rate for
\|\mathcal{I}_{3}\|^{2}
which is the same as
\|\mathcal{I}_{8}\|^{2}
and
\|\mathcal{I}_{9}\|^{2}
. Thus, it suffices to consider the following ratio as
p,q,T\to\infty
,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{I}_{3}\|^{2}/\|\mathcal{I}_{1}\|^{2}=O_{P}\big(1/p^{\delta_{r,1}}+Tq/p^{\delta_{r,1}+\delta_{r,k_{r}}}\big)=o_{P}(1),

by the rate assumption
Tq=o(p^{\delta_{r,1}+\delta_{r,k_{r}}})
. Therefore,
\mathcal{I}_{1}
is dominating over other terms in (
6.21
) and hence we have

\begin{split}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,j\cdot}&=\mathcal{I}_{1}+o_{P}(1)=\frac{1}{T}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})_{ij}+o_{P}(1)\\
&\xrightarrow{p}\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\ast}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})_{ij},\end{split}

(6.26)

where the last line used Lemma
4
and Lemma
5
in which
\mathbf{D}_{r}
and
\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\ast}
are defined, respectively. In the rest of the proof, we show

\sqrt{T\omega_{B}}\cdot\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\ast}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})_{ij}\xrightarrow{\mathcal{D}}\mathcal{N}\big(\mathbf{0},T^{-1}\omega_{B}\cdot\mathbf{D}_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\ast}\mathbf{\Xi}_{r,j}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\ast})^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{-1}\big),

with
\omega_{B}:=p^{2\delta_{r,k_{r}}-\delta_{r,1}}q^{2\delta_{c,1}-1}
and
\mathbf{\Xi}_{r,j}:=\plim_{p,q,T\to\infty}\text{Var}\big(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})_{ij}\big)
. We will adapt the central limit theorem for
\alpha
-mixing processes as depicted in Theorem 2.21 in
Fan and Yao, (2003)
. First, define
\mathbf{B}_{j,t}:=\sqrt{\omega_{B}}\cdot\mathbf{D}_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\ast}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}(\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})_{ij}
, and also let
\mathbf{b}_{e,t}:=\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}
,
\mathbf{b}_{\epsilon,il,t}:=\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{\epsilon,w}(\mathbf{X}_{\epsilon,t-w})_{il}
and
\mathbf{b}_{f,t}:=\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{f,w}\mathbf{X}_{f,t-w}
which are independent of each other by Assumption (E2).

Since we may write
\mathbf{B}_{j,t}=h(\mathbf{b}_{e,t},(\mathbf{b}_{\epsilon,il,t})_{i\in[p],l\in[q]},\mathbf{b}_{f,t})
for some function
h
, we conclude
\mathbf{B}_{j,t}
is
\alpha
-mixing using Theorem 5.2 in
Bradley, (2005)
. Observe that
\mathbbm{E}[\mathbf{B}_{j,t}]=\mathbf{0}
, and we show in the following that there exists an
m>2
such that
\mathbbm{E}[\|\mathbf{B}_{j,t}\|^{m}]\leq C
for some constant
C
,

\begin{split}\mathbbm{E}[\|\mathbf{B}_{j,t}\|^{m}]&\leq\omega_{B}^{m/2}\cdot\|\mathbf{D}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{m}\cdot\|\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\ast}\|_{F}^{m}\cdot\|\mathbf{Q}_{r}\|_{F}^{m}\cdot\mathbbm{E}\bigg\{\Big[\sum_{i=1}^{p}\Big(\sum_{l=1}^{q}E_{t,jl}\mathbf{A}_{c,h\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r,i\cdot}\Big)^{2}\Big]^{m/2}\bigg\}\\
&=O\Big((p^{2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{2\delta_{c,1}})^{m/2}\Big)\cdot\|\mathbf{D}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{m}=O(1),\end{split}

where we used Lemma
1
.2 and the definition of
\omega_{B}
in the second last equality, and Lemma
4
in the last equality. Theorem 2.21 in
Fan and Yao, (2003)
then applies, and hence

\sqrt{T\omega_{B}}\cdot\frac{1}{T}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\ast}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})_{ij}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{B}_{j,t}\xrightarrow{\mathcal{D}}\mathcal{N}\big(\mathbf{0},T^{-1}\omega_{B}\cdot\mathbf{D}_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\ast}\mathbf{\Xi}_{r,j}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\ast})^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{-1}\big).

Together with (
6.26
), we arrive at

(Tp^{2\delta_{r,k_{r}}-\delta_{r,1}}q^{2\delta_{c,1}-1})^{1/2}\cdot(\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,j\cdot})\xrightarrow{\mathcal{D}}\mathcal{N}\big(\mathbf{0},T^{-1}p^{2\delta_{r,k_{r}}-\delta_{r,1}}q^{2\delta_{c,1}-1}\cdot\mathbf{D}_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\ast}\mathbf{\Xi}_{r,j}(\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\ast})^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{-1}\big).

(6.27)

This completes the proof of Theorem
5
.
\square

Proof of Theorem
6
.

We only prove the scenario for
Tq=o(p^{\delta_{r,1}+\delta_{r,k_{r}}})
, which is on showing the constructed estimator for the row loading estimator is consistent. Note
\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}
consistently estimates
\mathbf{D}_{r}
by Lemma
4
, and
\mathbf{H}_{r}
consistently estimates
\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\ast}
by Lemma
5
. Then observe that

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\mathbf{H}_{r}\textnormal{Var}\Big\{\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})_{ij}\Big\}\mathbf{H}_{a}^{\prime}=\textnormal{Var}\Big\{\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})_{ij}\Big\}\\
&=\textnormal{Var}\Big\{\sum_{i=1}^{p}\Big(T^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{r}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{Z,s}\mathbf{Q}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{F}_{Z,s}^{\prime}\Big)\mathbf{Q}_{r,i\cdot}\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})_{ij}\Big\}\\
&=\textnormal{Var}\Big\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\Big(T^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{s}\mathbf{C}_{s,i\cdot}\Big)(\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime})_{ij}\Big\}.\end{split}

By Theorem
2
,
(\widehat{\mu}_{t},\widehat{\bm{\alpha}}_{t},\widehat{\bm{\beta}}_{t})
is consistent for
(\mu_{t},\bm{\alpha}_{t},\bm{\beta}_{t})
. By Theorem
3
and the rate assumption in the statement of Theorem
6
,
\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{t}
is consistent for
\mathbf{C}_{t}
and hence
\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{t}
is consistent for
\mathbf{E}_{t}
. Thus, we conclude that
\widehat{\bm{\Sigma}}_{r,j}^{HAC}
is estimating
\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{\Xi}_{r,j}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\prime}
consistently (
Newey and West, (1987)
) and hence result 1 is implied. Result 2 then follows, and results 3 and 4 can be shown similarly (details omitted). This completes the proof of the Theorem
6
.
\square

Proof of Theorem
7
.

Combining (
3.4
) and (
3.6
), we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{t}=\mathbf{M}_{p}\mathbf{Y}_{t}\mathbf{M}_{q}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{t}=\mathbf{M}_{p}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{M}_{q}+\mathbf{M}_{p}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{M}_{q}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{t}

\displaystyle=(\mathbf{C}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{t})+\mathbf{M}_{p}\mathbf{A}_{e,r}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}\mathbf{A}_{e,c}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{q}+\mathbf{M}_{p}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}\circ\bm{\epsilon}_{t})\mathbf{M}_{q},

where the second line comes from (IC1) being satisfied, so that
\mathbf{M}_{p}\mathbf{A}_{r}=\mathbf{A}_{r}
and
\mathbf{M}_{q}\mathbf{A}_{c}=\mathbf{A}_{c}
. Hence

\displaystyle q^{-1}(\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{t}^{\prime})_{ii}-q^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{6}I_{i},\text{ where }

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle:=q^{-1}\{\mathbf{M}_{p}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}\circ\bm{\epsilon}_{t})\mathbf{M}_{q}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}\circ\bm{\epsilon}_{t})^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{p}\}_{ii}-q^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}^{2},

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle:=q^{-1}\{(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{t}-\mathbf{C}_{t})(\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{t}-\mathbf{C}_{t})^{\prime}\}_{ii},

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle:=q^{-1}\{\mathbf{M}_{p}\mathbf{A}_{e,r}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}\mathbf{A}_{e,c}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{q}\mathbf{A}_{e,c}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,r}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{p}\}_{ii},

\displaystyle I_{4}

\displaystyle:=O_{P}(q^{-1}\{(\mathbf{C}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{t})\mathbf{M}_{q}\mathbf{A}_{e,c}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,r}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{p}\}_{ii}),

\displaystyle I_{5}

\displaystyle:=O_{P}(q^{-1}\{(\mathbf{C}_{t}-\widehat{\mathbf{C}}_{t})\mathbf{M}_{q}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}\circ\bm{\epsilon}_{t})^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{p}\}_{ii}),

\displaystyle I_{6}

\displaystyle:=O_{P}(q^{-1}\{\mathbf{M}_{p}\mathbf{A}_{e,r}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}\mathbf{A}_{e,c}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{q}(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}\circ\bm{\epsilon}_{t})^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{p}\}_{ii}).

By an assumption in the statement of the theorem, we have
I_{2}=o_{P}(q^{-1})
. Since
\|\mathbf{M}_{p}\mathbf{A}_{e,r}\|_{1}\leq\|\mathbf{M}_{p}\|_{1}\|\mathbf{A}_{e,r}\|_{1}=O(1)
, with the finiteness of
k_{r}
and
k_{c}
, we have
I_{3},I_{6}=O_{P}(q^{-1})
, and by the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,
I_{4}=O_{P}(I_{2}^{1/2}\cdot I_{6}^{1/2})=o_{P}(q^{-1})
.
Writing
\eta_{t,ij}:=(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}\circ\bm{\epsilon}_{t})_{ij}
, we can expand

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{q}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}^{2}(\epsilon_{t,ij}^{2}-1)-\frac{2}{q}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\bigg(\frac{1}{p}\sum_{\ell=1}^{p}\eta_{t,\ell j}\bigg)\eta_{t,ij}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2}{q}\bigg(\frac{\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\bm{\eta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}}{pq}\bigg)\sum_{j=1}^{q}\eta_{t,ij}+\frac{1}{q}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\bigg(\frac{1}{p}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\eta_{t,ij}\bigg)^{2}-\bigg(\frac{\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\bm{\eta}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}}{pq}\bigg)^{2}-\bigg(\frac{1}{q}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\eta_{t,ij}\bigg)^{2},

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{q}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}^{2}(\epsilon_{t,ij}^{2}-1)-\frac{2}{pq}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\eta_{t,ij}^{2}-\frac{2}{pq}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\sum_{\ell\neq i}\eta_{t,\ell j}\eta_{t,ij}

\displaystyle\quad+O_{P}(q^{-1}p^{-1/2})+O_{P}(p^{-1})+O_{P}(p^{-1}q^{-1})+O_{P}(q^{-1})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{q}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}^{2}(\epsilon_{t,ij}^{2}-1)(1+o_{P}(1)),

where all rates of convergence above are obtained from applying the Markov inequality. Hence

\displaystyle\frac{(\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{t}^{\prime})_{ii}-\sum_{j=1}^{q}\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}^{2}}{\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{q}\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}^{4}\textnormal{Var}(\epsilon_{t,ij}^{2})}}

\displaystyle=\frac{q^{-1}(\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{t}^{\prime})_{ii}-q^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}^{2}}{\sqrt{q^{-2}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\textnormal{Var}(\epsilon_{t,ij}^{2})\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}^{4}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{q^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}^{2}(\epsilon_{t,ij}^{2}-1)(1+o_{P}(1))}{\sqrt{q^{-2}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\textnormal{Var}(\epsilon_{t,ij}^{2})\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}^{4}}}

\displaystyle\xrightarrow{\mathcal{D}}Z_{i}\xrightarrow{\mathcal{D}}\mathcal{N}(0,1),

where the last line follows from Theorem 1 in
Ayvazyan and Ulyanov, (2023)
, with

Z_{i}:=\frac{q^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}^{2}(\epsilon_{t,ij}^{2}-1)}{\sqrt{q^{-2}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\textnormal{Var}(\epsilon_{t,ij}^{2})\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}^{4}}},\;\;\;i\in[p],

so that we can easily see that the
Z_{i}
’s are independent of each other by Assumption (E1). The proof for
(\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{t}^{\prime})_{ii}
completes since from the calculations for
I_{1}
, we see that

I_{5}=O_{P}(I_{2}^{1/2}\cdot 1)=o_{P}(q^{-1/2}).

For
(\check{\mathbf{E}}_{t}\check{\mathbf{E}}_{t}^{\prime})_{ii}
under
H_{0}
, note that we have

\check{\mathbf{E}}_{t}=(\mathbf{C}_{t}-\check{\mathbf{C}}_{t})+\mathbf{A}_{e,r}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}\mathbf{A}_{e,c}^{\prime}+(\bm{\Sigma}_{\epsilon}\circ\bm{\epsilon}_{t}),

with the rate for
\check{C}_{t,ij}-C_{t,ij}
the same as that for
\widehat{C}_{t,ij}-C_{t,ij}
since the estimation procedure for FM is essentially the same with the same assumptions on the factor loadings (apart from (IC1) which is not important for FM), the factors and the noise. Hence the proof we employed so far can be replicated with
\mathbf{M}_{p}
and
\mathbf{M}_{q}
replaced by the corresponding sized identity matrices, and we arrive at the same conclusion with the same
Z_{i}
’s.

For
(\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{t}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{t})_{jj}
under both
H_{0}
and
H_{1}
and
(\check{\mathbf{E}}_{t}^{\prime}\check{\mathbf{E}}_{t})_{jj}
under
H_{0}
, the proofs are parallel to that for
(\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{t}^{\prime})_{ii}
, and we omit them here.
\square

Proof of Corollary
8
.

Since both
(\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{E}}_{t})_{ii}
and
(\check{\mathbf{E}}_{t}\check{\mathbf{E}}_{t}^{\prime})_{ii}
are distributed asymptotically the same under
H_{0}
by Theorem
7
for each
i\in[p]
and
t\in[T]
, we have that
x_{\alpha,t}
and
y_{\alpha,t}
are asymptotically distributed the same for each
t\in[T]
under
H_{0}
. Hence
\widehat{q}_{x,\alpha}
and
\widehat{q}_{y,\alpha}
are asymptotically the same where

\widehat{q}_{y,\alpha}(\theta):=\inf\{c\;|\;\mathbbm{F}_{y,\alpha}(c)\geq\theta\}
. This implies that under
H_{0}
, for each
t\in[T]
,

\displaystyle 1-\theta\geq\mathbbm{P}_{y,\alpha}(y_{\alpha,t}\geq\widehat{q}_{y,\alpha}(\theta))\rightarrow\mathbbm{P}_{y,\alpha}(y_{\alpha,t}\geq\widehat{q}_{x,\alpha}(\theta)).

The other inequality is similarly proved, and is omitted here.
\square

Proof of Theorem
9
.

First consider
\widehat{k}_{r}
, i.e. result 1 in Theorem
9
. For
j\in[k_{r}]
, first consider

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\lambda_{j}\Big(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\Big)=\lambda_{j}\Big(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{A}_{r}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\Big)=\lambda_{j}\Big(\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r}\cdot\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\Big)\\
&\asymp_{P}\text{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c})\cdot\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r})=\|\mathbf{A}_{c}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\lambda_{j}(\bm{\Sigma}_{A,r}^{1/2}\mathbf{Z}_{r}\bm{\Sigma}_{A,r}^{1/2})\asymp q^{\delta_{c,1}}\cdot\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{Z}_{r})=p^{\delta_{r,j}}q^{\delta_{c,1}},\end{split}

(6.28)

where in the second line, we used Assumption (F1) in the first step and Assumption (L1) in the second. For the second last step, we used Theorem 1 of
Ostrowski, (1959)
on the eigenvalues of a congruent transformation
\bm{\Sigma}_{A,r}^{1/2}\mathbf{Z}_{r}\bm{\Sigma}_{A,r}^{1/2}
of
\mathbf{Z}_{r}
, where we further used Assumption (L1) that all eigenvalues of
\bm{\Sigma}_{A,r}
are bounded away from
0
and infinity.

Since we have
T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}=T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{r,t}
from (
6.15
), it holds by Weyl’s inequality that for
j\in[k_{r}]
,

\Big|\lambda_{j}\Big(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}\Big)-\lambda_{j}\Big(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\Big)\Big|\leq\Big\|\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{r,t}\Big\|=o_{P}(\omega_{r}),

(6.29)

where
\omega_{r}=p^{\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{\delta_{c,1}}
as defined in Lemma
3
, and the last equality used
\bm{\gamma}^{\prime}(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{r,t})\bm{\gamma}=o_{P}(\omega_{r})
for any unit vector
\bm{\gamma}\in\mathbbm{R}^{p}
from the proof of Lemma
3
. With our choice of
\xi_{r}
, we also have

\xi_{r}/\omega_{r}\asymp p^{1-\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{1-\delta_{c,1}}\big[(Tq)^{-1/2}+p^{-1/2}\big]=o(1),

(6.30)

where we used Assumption (R1) in the last equality. Hence for
k_{r}>1
, if
j\in[k_{r}-1]
, using (
6.29
) and (
6.30
) we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\frac{\lambda_{j+1}\big(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}\big)+\xi_{r}}{\lambda_{j}\big(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}\big)+\xi_{r}}\\
&\leq\frac{\lambda_{j+1}\big(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\big)+\xi_{r}+\big|\lambda_{j+1}\big(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}\big)-\lambda_{j+1}\big(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\big)\big|}{\lambda_{j}\big(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\big)+\xi_{r}-\big|\lambda_{j}\big(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}\big)-\lambda_{j}\big(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\big)\big|}\\
&=\frac{\lambda_{j+1}\big(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\big)+o_{P}(\omega_{r})}{\lambda_{j}\big(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\big)+o_{P}(\omega_{r})}=\frac{\lambda_{j+1}\big(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\big)}{\lambda_{j}\big(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\big)}\big(1+o_{P}(1)\big)\asymp_{P}p^{\delta_{r,j+1}-\delta_{r,j}},\end{split}

(6.31)

where the last line used (
6.28
). Moreover, for any
j\in[k_{r}-1]
,

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\frac{\lambda_{k_{r}+1}\big(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}\big)+\xi_{r}}{\lambda_{k_{r}}\big(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}\big)+\xi_{r}}=\frac{\lambda_{k_{r}+1}\big(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}\big)+\xi_{r}}{\omega_{r}(1+o_{P}(1))}=O_{P}\Big\{\frac{\lambda_{k_{r}+1}\big(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}\big)}{\omega_{r}}+\frac{\xi_{r}}{\omega_{r}}\Big\}\\
&=O_{P}\Big\{O_{P}\Big(pq[(Tq)^{-1/2}+p^{-1/2}]/\omega_{r}\Big)+\xi_{r}/\omega_{r}\Big\}=O_{P}(\xi_{r}/\omega_{r})=o_{P}(p^{\delta_{r,j+1}-\delta_{r,j}}),\end{split}

(6.32)

where we used (
6.30
) and the proof of Lemma
3
in the first equality, our choice of
\xi_{r}
in the second last, and the extra rate assumption in the statement of the theorem in the last. In the third equality, we used the following (which will be shown at the end of the this proof),

\lambda_{j}\Big(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\Big)=O_{P}(T^{-1/2}pq^{1/2}+p^{1/2}q),\;\;\;j=k_{r}+1,\dots,p.

(6.33)

Hence for
j=k_{r}+1,\dots,\lfloor p/2\rfloor
(true also for
k_{r}=1
),

\frac{\lambda_{j+1}\big(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}\big)+\xi_{r}}{\lambda_{j}\big(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}\big)+\xi_{r}}\geq\frac{\xi_{r}/\omega_{r}}{O_{P}(\xi_{r}/\omega_{r})+\xi_{r}/\omega_{r}}\geq\frac{1}{C}

(6.34)

in probability for some generic positive constant
C
, where we used again our choice of
\xi_{r}
and (
6.33
) in the first inequality. Combining (
6.31
), (
6.32
) and (
6.34
), we may conclude our proposed
\widehat{k}_{r}
is consistent for
k_{r}
.

If
k_{r}=1
, then from our choice of
\xi_{r}
, (
6.32
) becomes

\frac{\lambda_{k_{r}+1}\big(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}\big)+\xi_{r}}{\lambda_{k_{r}}\big(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}\big)+\xi_{r}}=O_{P}(\xi_{r}/\omega_{r})=o_{P}(1).

Together with (
6.34
) which holds true for
k_{r}=1
, we can also conclude
\widehat{k}_{r}
is consistent for
k_{r}
.

It remains to show (
6.33
). To this end, from (
6.28
) and (
6.29
), the first
k_{r}
eigenvalues of
T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}
coincides with those of
T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}
asymptotically, so that the first
k_{r}
eigenvectors corresponding to
T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}
coincides with those for
T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}
asymptotically as
T,p\rightarrow\infty
, which are necessarily in
\mathcal{N}^{\perp}:=\text{Span}(\mathbf{Q}_{r})
, the linear span of the columns of
\mathbf{Q}_{r}
. This means that the
(k_{r}+1)
-th largest eigenvalue of
T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}
and beyond will asymptotically have eigenvectors in
\mathcal{N}
, the orthogonal complement of
\mathcal{N}^{\perp}
. Then for any unit vectors
\bm{\gamma}\in\mathcal{N}
, we have from (
6.51
) and Lemma
2
that

\displaystyle\bm{\gamma}^{\prime}\Big(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}\Big)\bm{\gamma}=\bm{\gamma}^{\prime}\Big(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{r,t}\Big)\bm{\gamma}=O_{P}\Big(T^{-1}\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{r,t}\Big\|_{F}\Big)=O_{P}(T^{-1/2}pq^{1/2}+p^{1/2}q),

which is equivalent to (
6.33
). This completes the proof of Theorem
9
.
\square

6.2
Lemmas and proofs

As we have the same factor structure as
Cen and Lam, (2024)
, we list the following Lemma
1
for further use and refer readers to
Cen and Lam, (2024)
for the proof in details.

Lemma 1

Let Assumptions (F1), (E1) and (E2) hold. Then

1.

(Weak correlation of noise
\mathbf{E}_{t}
across different rows, columns and times) there exists some positive constant
C<\infty
so that for any
t\in[T],i,j\in[p],h\in[q]
,

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{p}\sum_{l=1}^{q}\Big|\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,ih}E_{t,kl}]\Big|\leq C,

\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{q}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\Big|\textnormal{cov}(E_{t,ih}E_{t,jh},E_{s,il}E_{s,jl})\Big|\leq C.

2.

(Weak dependence between factor
\mathbf{F}_{t}
and noise
\mathbf{E}_{t}
) there exists some positive constant
C<\infty
so that for any
j\in[p],i\in[q]
, and any deterministic vectors
\mathbf{u}\in\mathbbm{R}^{k_{r}}
and
\mathbf{v}\in\mathbbm{R}^{k_{c}}
with constant magnitudes,

\mathbbm{E}\Bigg(\frac{1}{(qT)^{1/2}}\sum_{h=1}^{q}\sum_{t=1}^{T}E_{t,jh}\mathbf{u}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{v}\Bigg)^{2}\leq C,\;\;\;\mathbbm{E}\Bigg(\frac{1}{(pT)^{1/2}}\sum_{h=1}^{p}\sum_{t=1}^{T}E_{t,hi}\mathbf{v}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{u}\Bigg)^{2}\leq C.

3.

(Further results on factor
\mathbf{F}_{t}
) for any
t\in[T]
, all elements in
\mathbf{F}_{t}
are independent of each other, with mean
0
and unit variance. Moreover,

\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\xrightarrow{p}\bm{\Sigma}_{r}:=k_{c}\mathbf{I}_{k_{r}},\;\;\;\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\xrightarrow{p}\bm{\Sigma}_{c}:=k_{r}\mathbf{I}_{k_{c}},

with the number of factors
k_{r}
and
k_{c}
fixed as
\min\{T,p,q\}\to\infty
.

Lemma 2

(Bounding
\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{r,t}
) Under Assumptions (F1), (L1), (E1) and (E2), it holds that

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}(Tp^{1+\delta_{r,1}}q),

(6.35)

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}(Tp^{2}q+T^{2}pq^{2}),

(6.36)

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}(Tp^{3}q^{2}+T^{2}p^{2}q^{2}),

(6.37)

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}(Tp^{3+\delta_{r,1}}q),

(6.38)

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}(Tp^{4}q+T^{2}p^{3}q^{2}),

(6.39)

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}(Tp^{2}q^{2}+T^{2}pq^{2}),

(6.40)

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p})^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}(T^{2}p^{4}q^{2}),

(6.41)

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}(Tp^{6}q+T^{2}p^{5}q^{2}).

(6.42)

Thus, with
\mathbf{R}_{r,t}
defined in (
6.15
), we have

\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{r,t}\Big\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}(Tp^{2}q+T^{2}pq^{2}).

Proof of Lemma
2
.
Using
\mathbf{C}_{t}=\mathbf{A}_{r}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}
, we have (
6.35
) holds as follows,

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}=\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{A}_{r}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}=\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\Bigg(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{A}_{r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t,l\cdot}\Bigg)^{2}\\
&=\sum_{i=1}^{p}\|\mathbf{A}_{r,i\cdot}\|^{2}\cdot\sum_{l=1}^{p}\Bigg(\sum_{h=1}^{q}\sum_{t=1}^{T}E_{t,lh}\frac{1}{\|\mathbf{A}_{r,i\cdot}\|}\mathbf{A}_{r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{A}_{c,h\cdot}\Bigg)^{2}=O_{P}\Big(Tp^{1+\delta_{r,1}}q\Big),\end{split}

where the last equality is from Assumption (L1) and Lemma
1
.

To show (
6.36
), first notice from Assumption (E1),

E_{t,ij}=\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}+\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}\epsilon_{t,ij}.

With Assumption (E2), we have

\text{cov}(E_{t,ij},E_{t,kj})=\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}\|\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}\|^{2}+\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{i=k\}},

and hence using Lemma
1
,

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\mathbbm{E}\Bigg[\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}\Bigg]=\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{k=1}^{p}\mathbbm{E}\Bigg[\Big(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{q}E_{t,ij}E_{t,kj}\Big)^{2}\Bigg]\\
&=\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{k=1}^{p}\Bigg[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{l=1}^{q}\text{cov}(E_{t,ij}E_{t,kj},E_{s,il}E_{s,kl})+\Big(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,ij}E_{t,kj}]\Big)^{2}\Bigg]\\
&=O(Tp^{2}q)+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{k=1}^{p}O\Big(T\cdot\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}\|\mathbf{A}_{e,c}\|_{F}^{2}+Tq\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{i=k\}}\Big)^{2}=O(Tp^{2}q+T^{2}pq^{2}).\end{split}

For (
6.37
), consider first

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\text{cov}(E_{t,ij}E_{t,kh},E_{s,lm}E_{s,kn})\\
&=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\text{cov}\Big[(\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}+\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}\epsilon_{t,ij})(\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot}+\Sigma_{\epsilon,kh}\epsilon_{t,kh}),\\
&(\mathbf{A}_{e,r,l\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{e,s}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,m\cdot}+\Sigma_{\epsilon,lm}\epsilon_{s,lm})(\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{e,s}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,n\cdot}+\Sigma_{\epsilon,kn}\epsilon_{s,kn})\Big]\\
&=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\text{cov}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot},\mathbf{A}_{e,r,l\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{e,s}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,m\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{e,s}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,n\cdot}\Big]\\
&+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,l\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{e,s}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,m\cdot}\Big]\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Big[\Sigma_{\epsilon,kh}\epsilon_{t,kh}\Sigma_{\epsilon,kn}\epsilon_{s,kn}\Big]\\
&+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{e,s}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,n\cdot}\Big]\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Big[\Sigma_{\epsilon,kh}\epsilon_{t,kh}\Sigma_{\epsilon,lm}\epsilon_{s,lm}\Big]\\
&+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,l\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{e,s}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,m\cdot}\Big]\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Big[\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}\epsilon_{t,ij}\Sigma_{\epsilon,kn}\epsilon_{s,kn}\Big]\\
&+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{e,t}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{e,s}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,n\cdot}\Big]\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Big[\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}\epsilon_{t,ij}\Sigma_{\epsilon,lm}\epsilon_{s,lm}\Big]\\
&+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\text{cov}\Big[\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}\epsilon_{t,ij}\Sigma_{\epsilon,kh}\epsilon_{t,kh},\Sigma_{\epsilon,lm}\epsilon_{s,lm}\Sigma_{\epsilon,kn}\epsilon_{s,kn}\Big]\\
&=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\text{cov}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot},\\
&\mathbf{A}_{e,r,l\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,s-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,m\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,s-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,n\cdot}\Big]\\
&+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,l\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,s-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,m\cdot}\Big]\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Big[\Sigma_{\epsilon,kh}\epsilon_{t,kh}\Sigma_{\epsilon,kn}\epsilon_{s,kn}\Big]\\
&+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,s-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,n\cdot}\Big]\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Big[\Sigma_{\epsilon,kh}\epsilon_{t,kh}\Sigma_{\epsilon,lm}\epsilon_{s,lm}\Big]\\
&+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,l\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,s-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,m\cdot}\Big]\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Big[\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}\epsilon_{t,ij}\Sigma_{\epsilon,kn}\epsilon_{s,kn}\Big]\\
&+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,s-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,n\cdot}\Big]\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Big[\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}\epsilon_{t,ij}\Sigma_{\epsilon,lm}\epsilon_{s,lm}\Big]\\
&+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\text{cov}\Big[\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}\epsilon_{t,ij}\Sigma_{\epsilon,kh}\epsilon_{t,kh},\Sigma_{\epsilon,lm}\epsilon_{s,lm}\Sigma_{\epsilon,kn}\epsilon_{s,kn}\Big].\end{split}

(6.43)

Consider the six terms in the last equality above, we have the first term as

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\text{cov}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot},\\
&\mathbf{A}_{e,r,l\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,s-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,m\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,s-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,n\cdot}\Big]\\
&=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}^{2}\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,n\cdot}\Big]\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,l\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,m\cdot}\Big]\\
&+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}^{2}\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,l\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,m\cdot}\Big]\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,n\cdot}\Big]\\
&+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}^{4}\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,l\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,m\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,n\cdot}\Big]\\
&-\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}^{4}\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot}\Big]\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,l\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,m\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,n\cdot}\Big]\\
&=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,n\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,l\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,m\cdot}\\
&+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,l\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,m\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,n\cdot}\\
&+O(1)\cdot\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}^{4}\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}\|^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{e,r,l\cdot}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{e,c,m\cdot}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{A}_{e,c,n\cdot}\|\\
&-\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}^{4}\cdot\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,m\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,l\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,n\cdot},\end{split}

(6.44)

where we used (E2) in the last equality that each entry in
\{\mathbf{X}_{e,t}\}
is independent with uniformly bounded fourth moment. Similarly, the remaining terms in last equality of (
6.43
) are

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,l\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,s-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,m\cdot}\Big]\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Big[\Sigma_{\epsilon,kh}\epsilon_{t,kh}\Sigma_{\epsilon,kn}\epsilon_{s,kn}\Big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,l\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,m\cdot}\cdot\Sigma_{\epsilon,kh}\Sigma_{\epsilon,kn}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{h=n\}},

(6.45)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,s-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,n\cdot}\Big]\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Big[\Sigma_{\epsilon,kh}\epsilon_{t,kh}\Sigma_{\epsilon,lm}\epsilon_{s,lm}\Big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,n\cdot}\cdot\Sigma_{\epsilon,kh}\Sigma_{\epsilon,lm}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{k=l\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{h=m\}},

(6.46)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,l\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,s-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,m\cdot}\Big]\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Big[\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}\epsilon_{t,ij}\Sigma_{\epsilon,kn}\epsilon_{s,kn}\Big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,l\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,m\cdot}\cdot\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}\Sigma_{\epsilon,kn}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{i=k\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{j=n\}},

(6.47)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,t-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{e,w}\mathbf{X}_{e,s-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{e,c,n\cdot}\Big]\cdot\mathbbm{E}\Big[\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}\epsilon_{t,ij}\Sigma_{\epsilon,lm}\epsilon_{s,lm}\Big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,n\cdot}\cdot\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}\Sigma_{\epsilon,lm}\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{i=l\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{j=m\}},

(6.48)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\text{cov}\Big[\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}\epsilon_{t,ij}\Sigma_{\epsilon,kh}\epsilon_{t,kh},\Sigma_{\epsilon,lm}\epsilon_{s,lm}\Sigma_{\epsilon,kn}\epsilon_{s,kn}\Big]

\displaystyle=O(T)\cdot\Big(\mathbbm{1}_{\{i=l=k\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{j=h=m=n\}}+\mathbbm{1}_{\{i=l\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{j=m\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{h=n\}}+\mathbbm{1}_{\{i=l=k\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{j=n\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{h=m\}}\Big).

(6.49)

Using (
6.44
), (
6.45
), (
6.46
), (
6.47
), (
6.48
) and (
6.49
), we arrive at an expression for (
6.43
). Thus, (
6.37
) can be obtained as

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\mathbbm{E}\Bigg[\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}\Bigg]=p\sum_{k=1}^{p}\mathbbm{E}\Bigg\{\Bigg[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{q}E_{t,ij}\Big)\sum_{h=1}^{q}E_{t,kh}\Bigg]^{2}\Bigg\}\\
&=p\sum_{k=1}^{p}\Bigg[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\sum_{h=1}^{q}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{l=1}^{p}\sum_{m=1}^{q}\sum_{n=1}^{q}\text{cov}(E_{t,ij}E_{t,kh},E_{s,lm}E_{s,kn})+\Big(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\sum_{h=1}^{q}\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,ij}E_{t,kh}]\Big)^{2}\Bigg]\\
&=O(Tp^{3}q^{2})+p\sum_{k=1}^{p}\Big[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\sum_{h=1}^{q}(\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot}+\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{i=k\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{j=h\}})\Big]^{2}\\
&=O(Tp^{3}q^{2}+T^{2}p^{2}q^{2}).\end{split}

(6.50)

By (
6.35
) and (
6.36
), we can obtain (
6.38
) and (
6.39
), respectively as follows,

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}\leq\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}(Tp^{3+\delta_{r,1}}q),

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}\leq\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}(Tp^{4}q+T^{2}p^{3}q^{2}).

Similar to the proof of (
6.37
), we can show (
6.40
) by

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\mathbbm{E}\Bigg[\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}\Bigg]=\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{k=1}^{p}\mathbbm{E}\Bigg\{\Big[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{q}E_{t,ij}\Big)\Big(\sum_{h=1}^{q}E_{t,kh}\Big)\Big]^{2}\Bigg\}\\
&=\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{k=1}^{p}\Bigg[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\sum_{h=1}^{q}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\sum_{m=1}^{q}\sum_{n=1}^{q}\text{cov}(E_{t,ij}E_{t,kh},E_{s,im}E_{s,kn})+\Big(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\sum_{h=1}^{q}\mathbbm{E}[E_{t,ij}E_{t,kh}]\Big)^{2}\Bigg]\\
&=O(Tp^{2}q^{2})+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\sum_{k=1}^{p}\Big[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{j=1}^{q}\sum_{h=1}^{q}(\mathbf{A}_{e,r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,r,k\cdot}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,j\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{e,c,h\cdot}+\Sigma_{\epsilon,ij}^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{\{i=k\}}\mathbbm{1}_{\{j=h\}})\Big]^{2}\\
&=O(Tp^{2}q^{2}+T^{2}pq^{2}).\end{split}

From (
6.2
), we can obtain (
6.41
) such that

\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p})^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}=\Big[\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p})^{2}\Big]^{2}\cdot\Big\|\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}(T^{2}p^{4}q^{2}).

Lastly, from (
6.36
) we have

\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}\leq\|\mathbf{1}_{p}\|^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{1}_{p}\|^{2}\cdot\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}(Tp^{6}q+T^{2}p^{5}q^{2}).

From (
6.15
), we have

\begin{split}\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{r,t}\Big\|_{F}^{2}&=O_{P}\Bigg(\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}+\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}+(pq)^{-2}\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}\\
&+p^{-2}\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}+p^{-2}\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}+p^{-2}\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{1}_{q}\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}\\
&+(pq)^{-2}p^{-2}\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}(\mathbf{1}_{q}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p})^{2}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}+p^{-4}\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\mathbf{E}_{t}\mathbf{E}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}\mathbf{1}_{p}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}\Bigg)\\
&=O_{P}(Tp^{2}q+T^{2}pq^{2}),\end{split}

which completes the proof of Lemma
2
.
\square

Lemma 3

Let Assumptions (M1), (F1), (L1), (E1), (E2) and (R1) hold. Define
\omega_{r}:=p^{\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{\delta_{c,1}}
and
\omega_{c}:=q^{\delta_{c,k_{c}}}p^{\delta_{r,1}}
. We have

\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}=O_{P}(\omega_{r}^{-1}),\;\;\;\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{c}^{-1}\|_{F}=O_{P}(\omega_{c}^{-1}).

Proof of Lemma
3
.

It suffices to show the bound of
\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}
, since that of
\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{c}^{-1}\|_{F}
would be similar. First, we bound the term
\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}
by finding the lower bound of
\lambda_{k_{r}}(\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r})
. To do this, consider the decomposition

\displaystyle\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}=\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{r,t},

(6.51)

so that for a unit vector
\bm{\gamma}\in\mathbbm{R}^{p}
, we can define

\displaystyle S_{r}(\bm{\gamma})

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{\omega_{r}}\bm{\gamma}^{\prime}\Big(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}\Big)\bm{\gamma}=:S_{r}^{*}(\bm{\gamma})+\widetilde{S}_{r}(\bm{\gamma}),\;\text{ with}

\displaystyle S_{r}^{*}(\bm{\gamma})

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{\omega_{r}}\bm{\gamma}^{\prime}\Big(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\Big)\bm{\gamma},\;\;\;\widetilde{S}_{r}(\bm{\gamma}):=\frac{1}{\omega_{r}}\bm{\gamma}^{\prime}\Big(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{r,t}\Big)\bm{\gamma}.

Since
\|\bm{\gamma}\|=1
, we have by Lemma
2
,

|\widetilde{S}_{r}(\bm{\gamma})|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{\omega_{r}^{2}T^{2}}\Big\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{r,t}\Big\|_{F}^{2}=O_{P}\Big(T^{-1}p^{2(1-\delta_{r,k_{r}})}q^{1-2\delta_{c,1}}+p^{1-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{2(1-\delta_{c,1})}\Big)=o_{P}(1),

where the last equality used Assumption (R1). Next, with Assumption (F1), consider

\displaystyle\lambda_{k_{r}}\Big(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\Big)

\displaystyle=\lambda_{k_{r}}\Big(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{A}_{r}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\Big)\geq\lambda_{k_{r}}(\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r})\cdot\lambda_{k_{r}}\Big(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\Big)

\displaystyle\asymp_{P}p^{\delta_{r,k_{r}}}\cdot\lambda_{k_{r}}(\text{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c})\bm{\Sigma}_{r})\asymp_{P}p^{\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{\delta_{c,1}}=\omega_{r}.

With this, going back to the decomposition (
6.51
),

\displaystyle\omega_{r}^{-1}\lambda_{k_{r}}(\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r})

\displaystyle=\omega_{r}^{-1}\lambda_{k_{r}}\Big(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}\Big)\geq\omega_{r}^{-1}\lambda_{k_{r}}\Big(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\Big)-\sup_{\|\bm{\gamma}\|=1}|\widetilde{S}_{r}(\bm{\gamma})|\asymp_{P}1,

and hence finally
\big\|\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\big\|_{F}=O_{P}\big(\lambda_{k_{r}}^{-1}(\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r})\big)=O_{P}(\omega_{r}^{-1})
, which completes the proof of Lemma
3
.
\square

Lemma 4

(Limit of
\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}
and
\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{c}
) Let Assumptions (F1), (L1), (E1), (E2) and (R1) hold. With
\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r},\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{c}
and
\omega_{r},\omega_{c}
defined in Lemma
3
, we have

\displaystyle\omega_{r}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}\xrightarrow{p}\omega_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{D}_{r}:=\omega_{r}^{-1}\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c})\cdot\textnormal{diag}\{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r})\mid j\in[k_{r}]\},

\displaystyle\omega_{c}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{c}\xrightarrow{p}\omega_{c}^{-1}\mathbf{D}_{c}:=\omega_{c}^{-1}\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r})\cdot\textnormal{diag}\{\lambda_{j}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c})\mid j\in[k_{c}]\}.

Proof of Lemma
4
.

It suffices to show the limit of
\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}
, as the proof for
\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{c}
will be similar. We first show

\frac{1}{\omega_{r}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\xrightarrow{p}\text{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c})\cdot\omega_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{A}_{r}\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime},

(6.52)

where
\lambda_{k_{r}}(\text{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c})\cdot\omega_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{A}_{r}\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime})\asymp 1
. By Assumption (F1), we have

\mathbbm{E}\Big(\frac{1}{\omega_{r}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\Big)=\frac{1}{\omega_{r}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbbm{E}[\mathbf{A}_{r}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}]=\text{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c})\cdot\omega_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{A}_{r}\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime},

where we used the independence structure among elements in
\mathbf{F}_{t}
. Furthermore, for any
i,j\in[p]
,

\begin{split}\text{Var}\Big[\Big(\frac{1}{\omega_{r}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\Big)_{ij}\Big]&=\frac{1}{\omega_{r}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\text{cov}\bigg(\mathbf{A}_{r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{f,w}\mathbf{X}_{f,t-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{f,w}\mathbf{X}_{f,t-w}^{\prime}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{r,j\cdot},\\
&\mathbf{A}_{r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{f,w}\mathbf{X}_{f,s-w}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c}\Big(\sum_{w\geq 0}a_{f,w}\mathbf{X}_{f,s-w}^{\prime}\Big)\mathbf{A}_{r,j\cdot}\bigg)\\
&=\frac{1}{\omega_{r}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{w\geq 0}\sum_{l\geq 0}a_{f,w}^{2}a_{f,l}^{2}\cdot\text{Var}\bigg(\mathbf{A}_{r,i\cdot}^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{f,t-w}\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c}\mathbf{X}_{f,t-l}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r,j\cdot}\bigg)\\
&=\frac{1}{\omega_{r}^{2}T^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{w\geq 0}\sum_{l\geq 0}a_{f,w}^{2}a_{f,l}^{2}\cdot O(\|\mathbf{A}_{c}\|_{F}^{4})=O(T^{-1}p^{-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}})=o(1),\end{split}

where we used Assumption (F1) in the third last equality, and both (L1) and (F1) in the second last, which concludes (
6.52
). Then it holds that

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\Big\|\frac{1}{\omega_{r}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}-\text{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c})\cdot\omega_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{A}_{r}\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}\\
&\leq 2\cdot\Big\|\frac{1}{\omega_{r}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}-\frac{1}{\omega_{r}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}+2\cdot\Big\|\frac{1}{\omega_{r}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}-\text{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c})\cdot\omega_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{A}_{r}\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}\\
&=2\cdot\Big\|\frac{1}{\omega_{r}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{r,t}\Big\|_{F}^{2}+2\cdot\Big\|\frac{1}{\omega_{r}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}-\text{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c})\cdot\omega_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{A}_{r}\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\Big\|_{F}^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\Big(T^{-1}p^{2(1-\delta_{r,k_{r}})}q^{1-2\delta_{c,1}}+p^{1-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{2(1-\delta_{c,1})}\Big)+o_{P}(1)=o_{P}(1),\end{split}

where the second last equality used Lemma
2
and (
6.52
), and the last used Assumption (R1). Using the inequality that for the
i
-th eigenvalue of matrices
\widehat{\mathbf{A}}
and
\mathbf{A}
,
|\lambda_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{A}})-\lambda_{i}(\mathbf{A})|\leq\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}-\mathbf{A}\|\leq\|\widehat{\mathbf{A}}-\mathbf{A}\|_{F}
, we have for any
i\in[k_{r}]
,

|(\omega_{r}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r})_{ii}-(\omega_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{D}_{r})_{ii}|=o_{P}(1).

Thus,
\omega_{r}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}\xrightarrow{p}\omega_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{D}_{r}
. This completes the proof of Lemma
4
.
\square

Lemma 5

(Limit of
\mathbf{H}_{r}
and
\mathbf{H}_{c}
) Under Assumptions (F1), (L1), (E1), (E2), (R1) and (L2), we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{H}_{r}\xrightarrow{p}\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\ast}:=(\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c}))^{1/2}\cdot\mathbf{D}_{r}^{-1/2}(\bm{\Gamma}_{r}^{\ast})^{\prime}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2},

\displaystyle\mathbf{H}_{c}\xrightarrow{p}\mathbf{H}_{c}^{\ast}:=(\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r}))^{1/2}\cdot\mathbf{D}_{c}^{-1/2}(\bm{\Gamma}_{c}^{\ast})^{\prime}\mathbf{Z}_{c}^{1/2},

where
\mathbf{D}_{r},\mathbf{D}_{c}
are defined in Lemma
4
, and
\bm{\Gamma}_{r}^{\ast},\bm{\Gamma}_{c}^{\ast}
are the eigenvector matrices of
\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c})\cdot\omega_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\bm{\Sigma}_{A,r}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}
and
\textnormal{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r})\cdot\omega_{c}^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{c}^{1/2}\bm{\Sigma}_{A,c}\mathbf{Z}_{c}^{1/2}
, respectively.

Proof of Lemma
5
.

The proof of the two limits are similar, and hence we only show the probability limit of
\mathbf{H}_{r}
is
\mathbf{H}_{r}^{\ast}
. First, left-multiply
\omega_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}
on (
6.17
), we have

\begin{split}(\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r})(\omega_{r}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r})&=\omega_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\Big(T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}\widehat{\mathbf{L}}_{t}^{\prime}\Big)\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}\\
&=\Bigg[\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\omega_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{Q}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{-1/2}+\mathbf{R}_{r,\text{res}}\Bigg](\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}),\end{split}

where
\mathbf{R}_{r,\text{res}}:=T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\omega_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{R}_{r,t}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}(\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r})^{-1}
. This implies each column of
(\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r})
is an eigenvector of the matrix
\Big[T^{-1}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\omega_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{Q}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{-1/2}+\mathbf{R}_{r,\text{res}}\Big]
. Note that

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\omega_{r}^{-1}\Big[(\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r})^{\prime}(\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r})-\text{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c})^{-1}\cdot\mathbf{D}_{r}\Big]\\
&=\omega_{r}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}-\frac{1}{\text{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c})}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{\prime}\Big(\frac{1}{\omega_{r}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\Big)\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}+\frac{1}{\text{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c})}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}^{\prime}\Big(\frac{1}{\omega_{r}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{C}_{t}\mathbf{C}_{t}^{\prime}\Big)\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}-\text{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c})^{-1}\cdot\mathbf{D}_{r},\end{split}

whose Frobenius norm is
o_{P}(1)
by (
6.52
) and Lemma
4
. We arrive at
(\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r})^{\prime}(\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r})\xrightarrow{p}\text{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c})^{-1}\cdot\mathbf{D}_{r}
. Thus, the eigenvalues of
(\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r})^{\prime}(\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r})
are asymptotically bounded away from zero and infinity by Assumption (L1), and hence
\|(\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r})^{-1}\|_{F}=O_{P}(\|\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{-1/2}\|_{F})
. Therefore, we have

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{R}_{r,\text{res}}\|_{F}^{2}&=O_{P}(1)\cdot\Big\|\frac{1}{\omega_{r}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{R}_{r,t}\Big\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\|_{F}^{2}\cdot\|(\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r})^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}\\
&=O_{P}\Big(T^{-1}p^{2(1-\delta_{r,k_{r}})}q^{1-2\delta_{c,1}}+p^{1-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{2(1-\delta_{c,1})}\Big)=o_{P}(1),\end{split}

(6.53)

where we used Lemma
2
in the second last equality and Assumption (R1) in the last.

Denote by the following as the normalization of
\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}
,

\bm{\Gamma}_{r}:=(\text{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c}))^{1/2}\cdot(\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r})\mathbf{D}_{r}^{-1/2}.

Using the limit in (
6.52
), we have

\begin{split}&\hskip 14.22636pt\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\omega_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}\mathbf{Q}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{c}\mathbf{F}_{Z,t}^{\prime}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{-1/2}=\frac{1}{\omega_{r}T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}(\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r})(\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r})^{-1}\\
&\xrightarrow{p}\text{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c})\cdot\omega_{r}^{-1}(\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r})(\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r})(\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r})^{-1}=\text{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c})\cdot\omega_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\bm{\Sigma}_{A,r}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}.\end{split}

By (
6.53
), Assumption (L2) and eigenvector perturbation theory, there exists a unique eigenvector matrix
\bm{\Gamma}_{r}^{\ast}
of
\text{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c})\cdot\omega_{r}^{-1}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\bm{\Sigma}_{A,r}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}
such that
\|\bm{\Gamma}_{r}^{\ast}-\bm{\Gamma}_{r}\|=o_{P}(1)
. Thus,

\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{r}=(\text{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c}))^{-1/2}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{1/2}\bm{\Gamma}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{-1/2}\xrightarrow{p}(\text{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c}))^{-1/2}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{1/2}(\bm{\Gamma}_{r}^{\ast})^{\prime}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{-1/2},

and hence using (
6.52
) again, we obtain

\begin{split}\mathbf{H}_{r}&=\widehat{\mathbf{D}}_{r}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}\Big(\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{A}_{r}\mathbf{F}_{t}\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c}\mathbf{F}_{t}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\Big)\mathbf{Q}_{r}\bm{\Sigma}_{A,r}^{-1}\xrightarrow{p}\mathbf{D}_{r}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}\Big(\text{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c})\cdot\mathbf{A}_{r}\mathbf{A}_{r}^{\prime}\Big)\mathbf{Q}_{r}\bm{\Sigma}_{A,r}^{-1}\\
&=\text{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c})\cdot\mathbf{D}_{r}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{r}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2}\mathbf{Q}_{r}^{\prime}\mathbf{Q}_{r}\bm{\Sigma}_{A,r}^{-1}\xrightarrow{p}(\text{tr}(\mathbf{A}_{c}^{\prime}\mathbf{A}_{c}))^{1/2}\cdot\mathbf{D}_{r}^{-1/2}(\bm{\Gamma}_{r}^{\ast})^{\prime}\mathbf{Z}_{r}^{1/2},\end{split}

which completes the proof of Lemma
5
.
\square

Lemma 6

Under the assumptions in Theorem
3
, for any
j\in[p],l\in[q]
,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,j\cdot}\|^{2}=O_{P}\big(T^{-1}p^{\delta_{r,1}-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{1-2\delta_{c,1}}+p^{-3\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{2-2\delta_{c,1}}\big),

(6.54)

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{c,l\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{c}\mathbf{Q}_{c,l\cdot}\|^{2}=O_{P}\big(T^{-1}q^{\delta_{c,1}-2\delta_{c,k_{c}}}p^{1-2\delta_{r,1}}+q^{-3\delta_{c,k_{c}}}p^{2-2\delta_{r,1}}\big).

(6.55)

Proof of Lemma
6
.

For
\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,j\cdot}
, first consider the case when
Tq=o(p^{\delta_{r,k_{r}}+\delta_{r,1}})
. From (
6.27
) in the proof of Theorem
5
, we have

\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,j\cdot}\|^{2}=O_{P}\big((Tp^{2\delta_{r,k_{r}}-\delta_{r,1}}q^{2\delta_{c,1}-1})^{-1}\big).

(6.56)

Now suppose
Tq=o(p^{\delta_{r,k_{r}}+\delta_{r,1}})
fails to hold. From the decomposition of
\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,j\cdot}
in (
6.21
), the leading term among the expressions will be
\mathcal{I}_{3}
. It has rate

\|\mathcal{I}_{3}\|^{2}=O_{P}\big(T^{-1}p^{-2\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{1-2\delta_{c,1}}+p^{-3\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{2-2\delta_{c,1}}\big)=O_{P}\big(p^{-3\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{2-2\delta_{c,1}}\big),

where the last equality used the fact that
Tq=o\big(p^{\delta_{r,k_{r}}+\delta_{r,1}}\big)
does not hold. Thus we have

\|\widehat{\mathbf{Q}}_{r,j\cdot}-\mathbf{H}_{r}\mathbf{Q}_{r,j\cdot}\|^{2}=O_{P}\big(p^{-3\delta_{r,k_{r}}}q^{2-2\delta_{c,1}}\big).

(6.57)

Combining (
6.56
) and (
6.57
), we arrive at the statement of (
6.54
). The proof for (
6.55
) is similar and omitted here, which ends of the proof of Lemma
6
.
\square
